### [37] [Minute-Scale Training for Microrobot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00854)
*Yinghan Sun,Aoji Zhu,Xiang Ji,Yamei Li,Jiachi Zhao,Yun Wang,Li Zhang,Huijun Gao,Lidong Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于DRL的微型机器人导航学习框架，通过全向量化仿真器与任务塑形正则化奖励机制，将训练时间从数小时/天缩短至10分钟以内，同时支持零样本部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于DRL的微型机器人导航方法训练效率低下，需数小时至数天，严重阻碍快速实际部署与参数优化。

Method: （1）开发全向量化仿真器，包含超10,000个人工血管环境，并行化动力学、类LiDAR感知与可行性检查，实现约190,000次状态转移/秒；（2）提出任务塑形正则化（TSR）奖励框架，加速收敛、提升最终性能。

Result: 训练时间降至10分钟以内；TSR框架使动作变异降低至少33.7%，障碍物间隙提升至少2.1%；支持不同微型机器人类型与导航场景的零样本部署。

Conclusion: 该框架可大幅缩短设计周期，加速自主微型机器人的部署落地。

Abstract: Microrobots hold significant potential for various applications, where targeted navigation is a basic requirement. Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) has recently emerged as a powerful paradigm for fully autonomous microrobot navigation. Yet, current DRL-based approaches pay limited attention to learning efficiency and effectiveness, requiring hours to days for model training. Consequently, this impedes both rapid practical deployment and parameter optimization. To address these challenges, we present a learning framework that enables effective microrobot navigation policies to be trained within minutes. In the proposed framework, we develop a fully vectorized simulator with more than 10,000 artificial vascular environments, parallelizing dynamics, LiDAR-inspired perception, and feasibility checks across thousands of environments to achieve roughly 190,000 transitions per second. To achieve effectiveness in the fast training, we propose a task-shaping-regularization (TSR) reward framework. The TSR framework accelerates convergence, improves final performance, reduces action variation by at least 33.7%, and increases obstacle clearance by at least 2.1% across all evaluated scenarios. Results show that the proposed learning framework reduces training time to under 10 minutes, while supporting zero-shot deployment across distinct microrobot types and navigation scenarios. Collectively, this framework can substantially shorten the design loop and accelerate the deployment of autonomous microrobots.

</details>


### [38] [Bicycle Acrobatics with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00880)
*Shamel Fahmi,Arianna Ilvonen,Samuel Zapolsky,Yu-Ming Chen,Ravi Boggavarapu,Paul Drews,Ashwin Khadke,Dean Molinaro,Kaiyu Zheng,Alfred Rizzi,Gabriel Nelson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 通过强化学习使自行车机器人学会并组合多种动态杂技动作，并通过编排器实现长时域特技表演，在仿真和硬件上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自行车机器人速度快、能效高，但其简单的机械设计以及欠驱动、非完整动力学特性使得高敏捷机动难以实现。现有研究未能赋予自行车机器人类似腿足平台的高敏捷性。

Method: 采用多种强化学习范式（航点跟随、位姿到达、扭量跟踪、引导跟踪和运动模仿）训练策略；引入基于状态触发器的编排器（orchestrator），在策略间平滑切换，实现长时域特技组合。

Result: 在Ultra Mobility Vehicle（UMV）自行车机器人上验证：机器人可反复翻越1米高桌台、执行超过15次连续自主跳跃、应对未见过的多桌台配置、连续20次以上执行kip-up/跳跃/前空翻/kip-down组合、连续10次以上执行可转向的后轮平衡、侧向跳跃及单轮跳下等动作。

Conclusion: 强化学习可赋予自行车机器人以往主要与腿足平台相关的敏捷性水平，同时保留轮式运动的速度与效率优势，为自行车杂技运动奠定了基础。

Abstract: Bicycle robots are fast and energy efficient, but their simple mechanical design and their underactuated and non-holonomic dynamics make highly agile maneuvers difficult to achieve. Here, we use Reinforcement Learning (RL) to enable a bicycle robot to learn and compose a diverse repertoire of dynamic acrobatic stunts. Using different RL formulations such as waypoint following, pose reaching, twist tracking, guided tracking, and motion imitation, the robot acquires autonomous single and multi-table forward and lateral jumps, steerable jumps, front flips, kip-ups, kip-downs, driving, wheelies, bunny hops, and three-point turns. To coordinate these behaviors, we introduce an orchestrator that transitions between policies using state-dependent triggers, enabling robust long-horizon acrobatic stunts. We validate the approach on the Ultra Mobility Vehicle (UMV), a custom bicycle robot, in simulation and hardware. The robot repeatedly traverses tables up to 1 m high, performs more than 15 consecutive autonomous jumps while following waypoints, handles previously unseen multi-table configurations, executes continuous repertoires of kipups, jumps, flips, kip-downs, over more than 20 consecutive trials, and performs more than 10 consecutive autonomous and steerable repertoires of wheelies, lateral jumps, and single-wheel jump downs. These results demonstrate that RL can endow bicycle robots with levels of agility previously associated primarily with legged platforms while preserving the speed and efficiency of wheeled locomotion, establishing a foundation for bicycle acrobatics.

</details>


### [39] [Stipple: Real-Time Incremental Gaussian Splatting with Visual-Inertial Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00931)
*Kilian Northoff,Mateo de Mayo,Daniel Cremers*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将视觉惯性SLAM系统(Basalt)与增量式3D高斯泼溅(Brush)相结合的实时同步跟踪与重建方法，通过利用SLAM生成的位姿和稀疏点云信息替代传统3DGS的重型预处理，实现实时三维重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统3D Gaussian Splatting需要大量预处理和离线训练，无法满足机器人和XR领域对实时重建和即时反馈的需求；而VIO/VI-SLAM系统天然适合实时应用，两者互补但此前缺乏有效整合方案。

Method: 基于Basalt视觉惯性跟踪系统提供实时位姿估计和稀疏地图信息，结合Brush（基于Rust的GPU无关3DGS实现），设计增量式训练策略来替代传统3DGS的重型预处理和离线训练；提出多项实用改进以提升训练管线效率，使训练线程与跟踪线程并行实时运行。

Result: 实现了实时同步跟踪与三维重建，证明了SLAM与3DGS互补融合在实时重建场景中的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过挖掘SLAM与3DGS的互补特性，能够实现有前景的实时三维重建结果，为机器人、XR等需要即时环境感知与交互的应用提供了新思路。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) provides efficient rendering of photo-realistic scenes, but its heavy preprocessing and training steps make it a poor fit for applications that require real-time reconstruction in robotics or XR. This capability is important since it allows immediate feedback and interaction with new environments. Visual-inertial odometry (VIO) and simultaneous localization and mapping (VI-SLAM) systems, on the other hand, specifically target these real-time applications, which makes them a good choice for integration with 3DGS. We propose a new method that tracks and reconstructs simultaneously in real-time by leveraging an efficient visual-inertial tracking system based on Basalt together with a novel incremental method built on top of Brush, an efficient Rust-based GPU-vendor-agnostic implementation of 3D Gaussian Splatting. We show that many of the heavy preprocessing and training steps of 3DGS can be replaced with a more efficient incremental training strategy that has direct access to the information generated by the visual-inertial tracking system. Furthermore, we propose and combine multiple practical improvements to increase the efficiency of the training pipeline and adapt it to run in real-time, parallel to the tracking thread. This work highlights the value of exploiting the complementary nature of SLAM and 3DGS, and how that can lead to promising results for real-time 3D reconstruction.

</details>


### [40] [VertiAKD: Adaptive Off-Road Kinodynamics on Vertically Challenging Terrain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00945)
*Tong Xu,Chenhui Pan,Francesco Cancelliere,Xuesu Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VertiAKD 是一个统一的框架，用于在几何和语义复杂地形上跨异构车辆迁移和适配越野运动学知识。它学习共享的移动性表征，结合车辆配置、轨迹转移和地形特征，仅需一分钟新数据即可通过函数编码器初始化并在线精炼地形感知的运动学模型，无需基于梯度的重训练，在预测误差和轨迹跟踪上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越野自主移动机器人面临两大挑战：一是跨异构车辆平台的泛化，二是持续变化的地形条件。现有跨车辆适配方法通常假设平坦地形，而地形感知的运动学模型则需要针对特定平台的数据采集和重训练，缺乏跨车辆、跨地形的统一迁移与适配框架。

Method: VertiAKD 框架学习一个共享的移动性表征，联合编码车辆配置、轨迹转移以及局部高程和语义地形特征。对于新车辆在未知地形上的有限数据，VertiAKD 识别最相关的移动性描述符，通过函数编码器将其知识迁移以初始化地形感知运动学模型，随后基于流式观测数据在线周期性精炼，无需梯度重训练。在基于 Chrono 多物理引擎的 Verti-Bench 仿真器和 Verti-4-Wheeler 平台的五种物理配置上进行了评估。

Result: 仅使用一分钟的新轨迹数据和相关地形特征，VertiAKD 在多种未见车辆配置上的长时域预测误差相比直接移动性描述符迁移降低了最多 34.52%，相比竞争基线降低了 94.43%。在仿真和物理实验中均展示了鲁棒的闭环轨迹跟踪能力。

Conclusion: VertiAKD 验证了地形感知的跨车辆知识迁移对于越野导航中精确建模和可靠控制的显著效果，为异构车队在复杂地形上的自主移动提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Off-road mobility requires autonomous mobile robots to generalize across heterogeneous vehicle fleets and continuously changing terrain conditions. Existing cross-vehicle adaptation approaches generally assume flat terrain, while terrain-aware kinodynamic models often require platform-specific data collection and retraining. To this end, we propose VertiAKD, a unified framework for transferring and adapting off-road kinodynamic knowledge across diverse vehicles on geometrically and semantically complex terrain simultaneously. VertiAKD learns a shared mobility representation that jointly encodes vehicle configurations, trajectory transitions, and local elevation and semantic terrain features. Given limited data from a novel vehicle operating on unseen terrain, VertiAKD identifies the most relevant mobility descriptors and transfers their knowledge to initialize a terrain-aware kinodynamic model via function encoders, which is then periodically refined online from streaming observations without gradient-based retraining. We evaluate VertiAKD in the Verti-Bench simulator, built on the Chrono multi-physics engine, and on five physical configurations of the Verti-4-Wheeler platform. With only one minute of new trajectory data and associated terrain features, VertiAKD reduces long-horizon prediction error by up to 34.52% over direct mobility descriptor transfer across diverse unseen vehicle configurations and 94.43% over competing baselines. We further demonstrate robust closed-loop trajectory tracking in both simulation and physical experiments, highlighting the effectiveness of terrain-aware cross-vehicle knowledge transfer for accurate modeling and reliable off-road navigation.

</details>


### [41] [GraRe: Grasp Candidate Re-Ranking for Frozen 6-DoF Grasp Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00946)
*Jibao Yuan,Yuhui Zhao,Yinzhen Lv,Chao Xu,Shun Li,Chenxi Deng,Shaofei Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对6-DoF抓取检测器中检测置信度与抓取质量不一致的问题，本文提出GraRe方法，通过从候选属性、壳层分层局部几何和物体上下文中估计抓取质量，结合检测置信度对候选进行重排序，在GraspNet-1Billion上显著提升排序质量，并在真实机器人实验中验证了效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者在GraspNet-1Billion数据集上的分析表明，现有6-DoF抓取检测器的置信度评分与真实抓取质量之间存在严重的不对齐问题，导致许多高质量的抓取候选在执行阶段被排在过低位置而被忽略。由此引出核心动机：在不修改冻结检测器及其候选的前提下，将抓取候选重排序作为一个独立任务，改善候选排序质量。

Method: 提出GraRe（Grasp Re-ranking）方法：(1)将候选重排序作为冻结检测器的后处理独立任务；(2)从三个维度估计抓取质量——候选自身属性（如抓取宽度、方向等）、壳层分层局部几何（shell-stratified local geometry，即抓取点周围分层采样的几何特征）、物体上下文信息；(3)候选属性用于条件化局部几何和物体上下文表示；(4)使用Transformer融合三类特征；(5)将预测的质量分数与原始检测器置信度结合，产生最终排序。

Result: 在GraspNet-1Billion数据集上对三种冻结检测器进行实验，均取得一致提升，Average AP最高提升达13.60个百分点。真实机器人实验进一步展示了在杂乱场景中的鲁棒抓取能力。

Conclusion: 通过改进候选排序而非修改检测器本身，是一种实用且有效的增强冻结6-DoF抓取检测器的途径。GraRe方法可作为一种通用后处理模块，适用于多种检测器。

Abstract: Existing 6-DoF grasp detectors typically rank grasp candidates by detector confidence. However, our analysis on GraspNet-1Billion shows that detector confidence is often poorly aligned with grasp quality, causing successful grasp candidates to be ranked too low during execution. Motivated by this observation, we formulate grasp candidate re-ranking as a separate task for frozen detectors, aiming to improve candidate ordering without changing the detector or its grasp candidates. We propose GraRe, which estimates grasp quality from candidate attributes, shell-stratified local geometry, and object context. Candidate attributes condition the local geometric and object-context representations, and a Transformer fuses all three feature types. The predicted quality is combined with detector confidence to produce the final ranking. Experiments on GraspNet-1Billion with three frozen detectors show consistent improvements, with gains of up to 13.60 points in Average AP. Real-robot experiments further demonstrate robust grasping in cluttered scenes. These results show that improving candidate ranking provides a practical way to enhance frozen 6-DoF grasp detectors.

</details>


### [42] [FreqNav: Stage-Wise Frequency Routing for Object-Oriented Aerial Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00970)
*Yin Tang,Jiawei Ma,Jiahao Li,Hao Zhang,Zhemin Sun,Jianqiao Sun,Deyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FreqNav 是一个面向长程空中视觉语言导航的轻量级框架，通过频率路由机制动态分配视觉token，实现从空间布局到局部细节的感知优先级转移，在性能超越基线方法的同时实现了约3倍推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空中VLN方法在导航各阶段使用相同的视觉token，忽视了感知需求的动态变化——早期需要低频空间布局信息，后期需要高频目标细节信息，导致无关物体和背景杂波的干扰。

Method: 提出FreqNav框架：(1) 将长程空中导航建模为从空间结构到局部细节的频率偏好转移；(2) 频率Token路由器从双视角观测中选择阶段相关的视觉表征；(3) 相位依赖的定位模块通过显式监督锚定视觉证据；(4) 扩散Transformer预测平滑轨迹用于连续控制。

Result: FreqNav在性能上超越强基线方法，同时推理速度提升约3倍，并在真实世界部署中验证了其有效性、高效性和实用潜力。

Conclusion: FreqNav通过频率路由自适应感知框架有效解决了长程空中VLN中感知需求动态变化的问题，在效率与性能之间取得了优异平衡，展示了在真实空中自主导航场景中的应用前景。

Abstract: Object-oriented aerial vision-and-language navigation (VLN) requires searching for a described target and landing on it precisely, under long-horizon and closed-loop control. Guided by a target-descriptive instruction during navigation, perceptual priorities dynamically evolve: early-stage exploration prioritizes low-frequency spatial layout, and then shifts to high-frequency target details. Existing VLN methods model the varying perceptual requirements across navigation stages with identical visual tokens, leading to interference from irrelevant objects and background clutter. To this end, we therefore formulate long-horizon aerial navigation as a frequencypreference shift from spatial structure to local detail and propose FreqNav, a lightweight frequency-routing adaptive perception framework. Under a fixed computational budget, FreqNav dynamically reallocates visual tokens across frequency components according to the current navigation stage. A Frequency Token Router selects stage-relevant visual representations from dual-view observations, while a Phase-dependent Grounding Module anchors visual evidence through explicit supervision. A Diffusion Transformer then predicts smooth trajectories for continuous control. Experiments show that FreqNav outperforms strong baselines while achieving approximately 3x faster inference. Real-world deployment further demonstrates its effectiveness, efficiency, and practical potential for long-horizon aerial autonomy.

</details>


### [43] [RL Bootstrapping of OpenVLA-OFT for a Novel Robot Embodiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01013)
*Damir Nurtdinov,Alexei Kornaev,Alexander Maloletov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究在完全不使用特定机器人示范数据的情况下，通过纯强化学习（PPO+GRPO两阶段训练）将预训练VLA策略OpenVLA-OFT适配到一个全新的索驱动并联机器人（CDPR）上，实现了初步的语言条件化控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA策略适配新机器人通常需要该机器人的示范数据，这对形态与常见机械臂差异巨大的定制机器人（如索驱动并联机器人CDPR）构成了严重限制。作者试图探索一个更困难的设定：零示范（zero-demo）条件下实现VLA策略与新机器人形态的对齐。

Method: 采用两阶段强化学习训练：第一阶段使用PPO进行方向性运动基元（directional motion primitives）训练，使用仿真器中密集几何奖励；第二阶段从PPO检查点出发，使用GRPO继续训练，并扩展指令空间加入物体条件化指令（如"移动到某物体"）。全程不使用任何机器人特定示范数据。

Result: 在四个共享方向指令上，平均held-out成功率从PPO后的34.25%提升至PPO→GRPO后的53.50%，"左移"和"后退"指令提升尤为显著。GRPO阶段新引入8个目标物体的"移动到物体"指令，严格成功率为9.75%（39/400），但定性rollout显示机器人常能正确逼近目标，后期出现不稳定。整体仍未能实现鲁棒操控。

Conclusion: 虽然尚未达到鲁棒操控水平，但结果提供了更强证据表明：纯RL引导（bootstrapping）可以为全新形态机器人创建首个可用的语言条件化控制器，无需依赖任何机器人特定示范数据集。

Abstract: Adapting a pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) policy to a new robot usually assumes embodiment-specific demonstrations. This assumption is especially restrictive for custom robots whose morphology differs strongly from the manipulators seen in large robot datasets. We study a harder setting: zero-demo embodiment alignment of OpenVLA-OFT on a cable-driven parallel robot (CDPR) with a simple gripper and a previously unseen control interface. Instead of supervised fine-tuning, we use reinforcement learning in simulation with dense geometric rewards computed from simulator state. The training is performed in two stages: a PPO stage for directional motion primitives, followed by GRPO continuation from the PPO checkpoint with an expanded instruction space that includes object-conditioned commands. On the four shared directional instructions, the average held-out success rate improves from 34.25\% after PPO to 53.50\% after PPO$\rightarrow$GRPO, with especially large gains on \texttt{move left} and \texttt{move backward}. In the GRPO stage we additionally introduce \texttt{move to <object>} over eight target objects and obtain 39/400 = 9.75\% strict success, while qualitative rollouts frequently show correct target-directed approach behavior before late-stage instability. Compared with prior OpenVLA and OpenVLA-OFT results, which rely on demonstration datasets and mostly standard rigid-arm embodiments, our method uses no embodiment-specific dataset at all. The results do not yet establish robust manipulation, but they provide stronger evidence that RL-only bootstrapping can create the first usable language-conditioned controller for a genuinely novel embodiment.

</details>


### [44] [KING: Embodiment-Aware Kinematic Graph Neural Network for Unified Motion Representation of Legged and Wheeled Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01015)
*Taku Okawara,Aoki Takanose,Kenji Koide,Shuji Oishi,Masashi Yokozuka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: KING 是一种基于图神经网络(GNN)的运动学模型，通过将轮式和腿式机器人表示为统一的图结构，实现了跨不同机器人本体(embodiment)的高精度里程计估计，并可通过少量样本快速适配新机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的运动学模型通常针对单一机器人本体训练，泛化性差。由于不同机器人的本体感受测量（关节数量、接地元件类型等）的语义和结构差异很大，跨本体泛化极具挑战。

Method: 提出 KING (GNN-based Kinematic model)，将机器人本体显式地表示为统一图结构，使轮式和腿式运动学模型能用同一表示表达。模型仅需本体描述文件(URDF)和机载本体感受(编码器+IMU)，即可进行里程计估计。支持通过仅一分钟数据的少样本学习适配新机器人。

Result: KING 在涵盖多种机器人本体的数据集上训练后，实现了轮式和腿式运动学的统一表示，在真实环境中达到高精度里程计估计，并能通过少样本学习快速适配新机器人本体，无需从头重新训练。

Conclusion: KING 成功实现了跨轮式和腿式机器人的统一运动学里程计模型，解决了学习-based模型跨本体泛化的关键难题，在特征贫瘠环境下具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: Kinematic models provide reliable motion constraints for odometry estimation in featureless environments, where exteroceptive sensing degrades and IMU integration drifts. Learning-based kinematic models can achieve more accurate odometry estimation than model-based methods by capturing nonlinear effects; however, most existing learning-based models are trained on a single embodiment and generalize poorly to new embodiments. This generalization is difficult because the meanings and structures of proprioceptive measurements vary across embodiments, including the number of joints and ground-contact elements (e.g., wheels, feet). To address this challenge, we propose KING, a Graph Neural Network (GNN)-based kinematic model that explicitly incorporates robot embodiments by representing them as a common graph. We show that wheel and leg kinematic models can be expressed by a unified representation, enabling a single model for both wheeled and legged robots. Trained on datasets spanning diverse embodiments, KING provides a unified representation of wheeled and legged kinematics and achieves high-accuracy odometry estimation in real environments. KING estimates accurate odometry using only an embodiment description (e.g., a URDF file) and on-board proprioception (encoders and an IMU) and can be adapted to new robot embodiments through few-shot learning with only one minute of data, avoiding retraining from scratch on a new dataset for each robot. The project page is available at: https://smrg-aist.github.io/king_project_page/

</details>


### [45] [Sampling-Based Visibility Task Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01027)
*Stav Ashur,Avishai Sintov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对基于可视性的工具（如相机、闪光灯、定向天线等）的任务与运动规划（TAMP）问题，提出两种新的采样算法 VisPRM 和 VisRRT。VisPRM 利用可视完整性概念进行环境层次分解，高效采样具有目标视线可达的构型；VisRRT 则借助专用逆运动学求解器在关键时刻"扫视"目标方向，快速发现关键构型。仿真与物理实验表明，两种算法在成功率和运行速度上均优于传统 RRT、PRM 和 VIR 的改进版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统任务与运动规划方法通常假设末端执行器为夹爪等工具，其启发式函数和距离度量对具有视场（FOV）约束的可见性仪器（如传感器、相机、手电筒等）效果不佳。这类设备的独特性质使传统方法失效，因此需要专门为可视性约束场景设计新的采样规划算法。

Method: 提出两种算法：(1) VisPRM——利用"可视完整性"概念对环境进行层次化分解，在具有到目标的清晰视线（line-of-sight）的区域中高效采样构型；(2) VisRRT——集成专用逆运动学求解器，使机器人在有利时刻"扫视"目标方向，从而快速定位关键构型。

Result: 在仿真和物理实验中，VisPRM 和 VisRRT 相较于传统 RRT、PRM 及 VIR 的改进版本，均取得了更高的成功率和更快的规划运行时间。

Conclusion: 专门针对可视性约束设计的采样规划算法（VisPRM 和 VisRRT）能够有效克服传统方法在视野受限场景中的不足，为基于可视性仪器的机器人自主操作提供了更高效、更可靠的规划方案。

Abstract: Robot Task and Motion Planning (TAMP) algorithms enable autonomous operation by incorporating the specific functions and constraints of end-effector tools, such as grippers or soldering irons, directly into the planning process. In this paper, we explore sampling-based TAMP algorithms specifically designed for a critical subset of devices whose unique properties make traditional planning methods ineffective. Visibility-based instruments, such as exteroceptive sensors, cameras, flashlights and directional antennas, are essential across a vast array of human activities. The unique properties of these devices, and particularly, their field-of-view, render many widely used heuristics and distance metrics less effective. We introduce two new sampling-based algorithms, VisPRM and VisRRT, designed to tackle visibility-based tasks. VisPRM employs a hierarchical decomposition of the environment, leveraging the concept of visibility integrity, to efficiently sample configurations with a clear line-of-sight to the target. A specialized Inverse Kinematics solver enables VisRRT to "glance" in the direction of the target at opportune moments, facilitating the rapid discovery of key configurations. We show that VisPRM and VisRRT achieve a higher success rate and faster runtimes compared to adaptations of RRT, PRM and VIR, through both simulated and physical experiments.

</details>


### [46] [VLAGuard: A Framework for Evaluating and Mitigating Physical Attention Hijacking in Vision-Language-Action Robots within Wireless Sensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01028)
*Dongfu Yin,Jinquan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出VLAGuard框架，针对VLA机器人在无线传感器网络中面临的动作-视觉跨注意力劫持漏洞，包含攻击模块VASA和防御方案APFT。APFT通过稳定时空注意力并强化几何一致性，在仿真和真实环境中显著提升了鲁棒性，将OpenVLA失败率从100%降至25.9%，真实世界成功率从23%提升至67.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署在无线传感器网络边缘节点上的VLA机器人面临物理对抗威胁，现有的注意力机制存在被劫持的风险——攻击者可通过可打印的物理补丁严重干扰机器人的动作条件化跨注意力（cross-attention），导致策略失败。目前缺乏针对此类"策略关键的动作到视觉注意力劫持"的评估与防御手段。

Method: 提出了VLAGuard框架，包含两大组件：(1) VASA（Visuomotor Attention-guided Semantic Attack）：一种压力测试模块，利用可打印的对抗补丁来严重分散机器人的动作条件化跨注意力；(2) APFT（Attention-Protective Fine-Tuning）：一种防御性微调方法，通过稳定时空注意力分布并施加几何一致性约束来保护注意力通路，且推理时零额外开销。

Result: 在LIBERO仿真环境中，APFT将OpenVLA的失败率从100.0%降至25.9%；在2000次真实世界试验中，面对严重补丁攻击，APFT将平均成功率从23.0%提升至67.4%。

Conclusion: 保护注意力通路对于提升传感器网络中VLA驱动的边缘节点鲁棒性至关重要，APFT以零推理开销实现了显著的鲁棒性增益。

Abstract: Deploying Vision-Language-Action (VLA) robots as mobile edge nodes within wireless sensor networks (WSNs) requires robust protection against physical adversarial threats. We present VLAGuard, a framework to assess and mitigate a critical vulnerability: policy-critical action-to-vision attention hijacking. We first introduce a stress-test module, Visuomotor Attention-guided Semantic Attack (VASA), using printable patches to severely distract the robot's action-conditioned cross-attention. To counter this, we propose Attention-Protective Fine-Tuning (APFT), a defense that stabilizes spatiotemporal attention and enforces geometric consistency with zero inference overhead. Evaluations across simulated and physical WSN-assisted smart environments demonstrate significant robustness gains. APFT reduces the OpenVLA failure rate from 100.0% to 25.9% in LIBERO simulations. Furthermore, across 2,000 real-world trials, APFT improves the average success rate from 23.0% to 67.4% under severe patch attacks. This highlights that protecting attention pathways is important for improving the robustness of VLA-driven edge nodes in sensor networks.

</details>


### [47] [Diffusion-Based Body Schema Learning Enabling Abnormal-State Adaptation in Musculoskeletal Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01029)
*Kento Kawaharazuka,Shuhei Ikemoto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于扩散模型的肌肉骨骼机器人身体图式学习框架，通过梯度引导的去噪过程，在肌肉断裂、执行器卡死等异常状态下实现高维空间中传感器与执行器信号的一致性自适应估计，无需重新训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于自编码器或变分自编码器的身体图式学习方法将信号投影到低维潜在空间，仅能在训练分布内进行探索，难以处理训练数据中未包含的肌肉断裂、执行器卡死等分布外异常状态。

Method: 提出基于扩散模型的身体图式学习框架。将身体图式自适应形式化为梯度引导的去噪过程，扩散模型可在高维空间中通过迭代去噪直接估计物理一致的传感器与执行器值，在部分观测和约束条件下无需重新训练即可适应异常状态。

Result: 在肌肉骨骼机器人模型的仿真实验中验证了所提框架的有效性，方法能够在肌肉断裂和执行器卡死等异常条件下自适应估计合适的肌肉长度与肌肉张力。

Conclusion: 扩散模型为肌肉骨骼机器人的身体图式学习提供了一种无需重训练即可处理分布外异常状态的有效途径，相比传统潜在空间方法具有更强的鲁棒性和适应性。

Abstract: Musculoskeletal robots require an internal body schema that remains consistent under a wide range of physical state changes, including abnormalities such as muscle rupture and actuator jamming. Conventional approaches based on autoencoders or variational autoencoders learn average behaviors by projecting sensor and actuator signals into a low-dimensional latent space; however, exploration within the latent space alone has limited capability to handle out-of-distribution or abnormal states that are not included in the training data. To address this limitation, this study proposes a diffusion-based framework for body schema learning in musculoskeletal robots. Unlike generative models that operate through low-dimensional latent spaces, diffusion models can directly and iteratively estimate physically consistent sensor and actuator values in the high-dimensional space through a denoising process, even under partial observations and constraints, without requiring retraining. By formulating body schema adaptation as a gradient-guided denoising process, the proposed method enables adaptive estimation of appropriate muscle lengths and muscle tensions even under abnormal conditions such as muscle rupture and actuator jamming. The validity of the proposed framework is verified through simulation experiments using a musculoskeletal robot model.

</details>


### [48] [WAM-Diff2: Hierarchical AR-to-Diffusion Distillation for Highly Efficient Autonomous Driving VLA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01035)
*Zhihao Zhu,Hanlin Shang,Mingwang Xu,Feipeng Cai,Zhuolin He,Yaoyi Li,Jianhua Han,Hang Xu,Siyu Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WAM-Diff2 提出一种三阶段分层蒸馏策略，将预训练的自回归 VLA 通用模型转换为多任务离散扩散框架，在保持性能的同时实现最高 15.1 倍的推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归 VLA 模型面临高计算延迟和暴露偏差问题，而扩散策略虽能并行低延迟执行但缺乏视觉-语言推理能力。将预训练自回归通用模型转换为并行扩散模型可兼顾认知智能与执行效率，但因果注意力与双向注意力模式的不匹配以及优化目标的差异构成了巨大的架构挑战。

Method: 提出 WAM-Diff2，一种多任务离散扩散 VLA 框架，核心采用三阶段分层蒸馏策略：（1）逐块渐进式架构适配（block-wise adaptation），（2）逐块蒸馏（block-wise distillation），（3）跨尺度模型级蒸馏（model-wise cross-scale distillation），从而在保留基座模型语义基础的同时实现推理加速。

Result: 在驾驶理解、感知和规划基准评测中，WAM-Diff2 有效缓解了暴露偏差，性能与自回归基线持平；自回归到扩散的转换带来 2.8 倍解码加速，结合 FlashInfer 和 CUDA Graphs 等系统级优化后，总体加速达 15.1 倍。

Conclusion: 通过精心设计的分层蒸馏策略，将自回归 VLA 转换为离散扩散模型是可行且高效的，能够在保持多任务认知能力的同时显著降低推理延迟，为端到端自动驾驶的高效部署提供了新路径。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a prominent paradigm for end-to-end autonomous driving; however, their efficient deployment is severely constrained by high computational latency and exposure bias arising from sequential autoregressive decoding. Conversely, while specialized diffusion policies enable low-latency, parallel execution, training them from scratch typically yields narrow, single-task architectures that lack holistic visual-linguistic reasoning. Successfully transforming pre-trained autoregressive generalists into parallel diffusion models could combine multi-task cognitive intelligence with execution efficiency, yet this transition presents a formidable architectural challenge due to mismatched attention patterns (causal versus bidirectional) and divergent optimization objectives. To bridge this divide, we introduce WAM-Diff2, a multi-task discrete diffusion VLA framework powered by a three-stage hierarchical distillation strategy. By structuring the architectural shift through progressive block-wise adaptation, block-wise distillation, and model-wise cross-scale distillation, WAM-Diff2 preserves the underlying semantic foundations of the base model while accelerating inference. Extensive evaluations across driving understanding, perception, and planning benchmarks demonstrate that WAM-Diff2 effectively mitigates exposure bias and achieves performance parity with autoregressive baselines. Crucially, the autoregressive-to-diffusion transition yields a 2.8x decoding speedup, which scales to an ultimate 15.1x acceleration when combined with system-level optimizations including FlashInfer and CUDA Graphs.

</details>


### [49] [OC-VLA++: Monocular Geometry-Guided Cross-View Consistency for Viewpoint-Robust Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01066)
*Tianyi Zhang,Ziyang Gong,Zhenjie Yang,Zhe Qian,Haonan Duan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OC-VLA++ 通过引入几何引导的配对视图监督和显式的跨视图动作等变目标，解决了 OC-VLA 在有限相机覆盖下视角泛化不足的问题，使模型学会不同视角下动作预测应如何变换，从而显著提升未见视角的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OC-VLA 将机器人动作建立在相机坐标系中，使动作监督与视觉观察对齐，但单纯依赖相机空间定位仍会过拟合训练时的有限视角，导致在新视角下性能急剧下降。因此需要一种机制显式约束模型学习跨视角的动作变换关系。

Method: 提出 OC-VLA++，核心包含两部分：(1) 几何引导的配对视图监督——利用几何关系已知的两个视角对同一操作场景进行观测；(2) 显式的跨视图动作等变目标——训练模型使两个视角下的相机空间动作预测对应到同一个机器人坐标系动作，从而显式监督动作预测在视角间的变换方式，而非仅依赖图像级数据增强。

Result: 在有限相机覆盖条件下，OC-VLA++ 在未见视角上的泛化性能大幅提升，且随着相机位移增大，性能下降更为平缓（graceful degradation），显著优于仅依赖观察中心动作定位的基线方法。

Conclusion: 跨视图动作等变性是观察中心动作定位的有效补充，两者结合能显著提升机器人在真实部署中的鲁棒性，尤其是在训练相机覆盖有限的场景下。

Abstract: We propose OC-VLA++, an extension of OC-VLA for viewpoint generalization under limited camera coverage. While OC-VLA grounds robot actions in the camera coordinate system to align action supervision with visual observations, camera-space grounding alone can still overfit to the few viewpoints observed during training. OC-VLA++ addresses this limitation by introducing geometry-guided paired-view supervision and an explicit cross-view action-equivariance objective. Given paired observations of the same manipulation scene from geometrically related viewpoints, the model is trained such that their camera-space predictions correspond to the same robot-frame action. This objective explicitly supervises how action predictions should transform across viewpoints, rather than relying solely on image-level augmentation. Experiments demonstrate substantial improvements in unseen-view generalization under limited camera coverage, with performance degrading more gracefully under increasing camera displacement. These results establish cross-view action equivariance as an effective complement to observation-centric action grounding for robust real-world deployment.

</details>


### [50] [VespaSeg: A Resource-Aware Ground-then-Segment Pipeline for Referring Expression Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01077)
*Savindu Dilshan Wickramasinghe*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VespaSeg 提出了一种模块化的指代表达分割流水线，使用紧凑的视觉语言模型进行文本引导定位，再结合 MobileSAM 将预测框转换为掩码，在保持较高精度的同时显著降低了部署成本和显存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单体式指代表达分割模型部署成本高昂。作者旨在通过模块化设计，将语言条件定位与像素级掩码生成解耦，利用紧凑模型实现高效、低资源的部署方案。

Method: VespaSeg 流水线包含两个阶段：(1) 使用 Florence-2-base/large 或 Moondream2 作为 grounder，根据文本查询预测目标边界框；(2) 使用 MobileSAM 将预测框转化为分割掩码。作者还对 grounder 进行真值框适配微调，并探索了减少 Florence-2 输出 token 预算（从64降至32）对精度的影响。

Result: 在 RefCOCO 验证集（每记录取首个表达式，共3811条）上，适配后的 Florence-2-base 流水线达到 73.64 mIoU 和 84.60 IoU@0.5 精度。在 NVIDIA RTX 6000 Ada 上，缓存图像查询速度达 22.8 queries/s，平均显存占用仅 2.20 GB。500查询对比中，Florence-2-base（73.73 mIoU）与 Florence-2-large（72.82）精度相当，但速度为其 1.70 倍且显存少用 1.17 GB。消融实验显示真值框适配将 MobileSAM mIoU 从 82.22 提升至 86.61，token 预算缩减未损失精度。

Conclusion: 紧凑的模块化定位与分割方案在性能和效率之间取得了良好平衡，验证了该技术路线的可行性。但作者也指出，仍需在 RefCOCO 完整标准表达式划分上进行评估，并在实际部署硬件上验证。

Abstract: Referring expression segmentation requires language conditioned localization and pixel-accurate masks, but monolithic models can be costly to deploy. We present VespaSeg, a modular pipeline that grounds a text query with a compact vision-language model and converts the predicted box to a mask with MobileSAM. We study Florence-2-base, Florence-2-large, and Moondream2 grounders together with targeted adaptation of the grounding and segmentation stages. Under a repository-specific RefCOCO validation protocol containing the first expression for each of 3,811 referenced-object records, the adapted Florence-2-base pipeline obtains 73.64 mean intersection over union (mIoU) and 84.60 precision at IoU 0.5. On an NVIDIA RTX 6000 Ada GPU it processes 22.8 cached-image queries per second with 2.20 GB mean allocated GPU memory. A matched 500-query comparison gives 73.73 mIoU for Florence-2-base and 72.82 for Florence-2-large, while the base model is 1.70 times faster and uses 1.17 GB less allocated memory. Ablations show that ground-truth-box adaptation raises MobileSAM mIoU from 82.22 to 86.61 and that reducing the Florence-2 output-token budget from 64 to 32 preserves accuracy. These results support compact, modular grounding and segmentation, while also exposing the need for evaluation on the complete standard RefCOCO expression splits and deployment hardware.

</details>


### [51] [Sparse Meets Dense: Correspondence Guided Robotic Manipulation with Rigid-Deformable Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01083)
*Ziyu Zhu,Yue Chen,Xirui Liang,Hojin Bae,Yuran Wang,Zhen Yuan,Ruihai Wu,Hao Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合对应表示方法，结合稀疏关键点与稠密对应，用于解决家庭机器人中刚体-可变形体交互（如挂衣服、穿衣）的精细操作问题，支持单次演示迁移到新任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日常生活中的刚体-可变形体交互（如挂衣服、穿衣）涉及丰富的多点接触和复杂动力学，传统的6D位姿或结构点等物体中心表示缺乏任务特定信息，无法胜任此类任务。因此需要一种能够编码交互信息、同时支持高效跟踪与泛化的新型表示。

Method: 提出一种混合对应表示：(1) 结构感知、任务感知和交互感知的稀疏关键点——基于刚体和可变形体的全局结构生成，并通过局部交互接触进行筛选；(2) 在可变形体上构建稠密对应，用于在整个操作过程中精确跟踪稀疏关键点。两者结合，稀疏关键点编码任务特定信息用于精细操作，稠密对应确保高效跟踪和对新变形、形状及场景的泛化。

Result: 大量实验验证了该方法的有效性和广泛适用性，支持以极少演示实现对新任务的一次性迁移（one-shot transfer）。

Conclusion: 混合对应表示结合了稀疏与稠密表示的各自优势，有效解决了刚体-可变形体交互中信息编码与跟踪的难题，为家庭机器人操作此类任务提供了通用且高效的方案。

Abstract: Manipulation involving rigid-deformable interactions, such as hanging clothes or dressing humans, is common in daily life, making it essential for household robots. Compared to single-object manipulation or interactions between rigid bodies, these tasks are particularly challenging due to the rich multi-point contacts and the complex dynamics of the deformable bodies during interaction. Therefore, object-centric representations such as 6D poses or structural points without task-specific information become insufficient for these interactions. In this work, we propose a hybrid correspondence-based representation tailored for rigid-deformable interactions. First, to capture intricate interaction information, we introduce structure-, task-, and interaction-aware sparse keypoints. The keypoints are generated based on the global structures of both rigid and deformable objects, and filtered by their local interaction contacts. However, tracking these sparse keypoints through the interaction remains difficult due to the high-dimensional dynamics of deformable objects. Therefore, we further construct dense correspondences on the deformable objects for accurate keypoint tracking throughout the manipulation. This hybrid design combines the advantages of both representations: sparse keypoints encode rich, task-specific information for fine-grained manipulation, while dense correspondences ensure efficient tracking and generalization to novel deformations, shapes, and scenarios. Together, they enable one-shot transfer to new tasks with minimal demonstrations. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness and broad applicability of our method.

</details>


### [52] [CAAT: Contact-Aware Attention Scaling and Tactile Masking for Data-Efficient Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01102)
*Jiaming Jiang,Yuzhe Huang,Hao Liang,Pei Lin,Shengcheng Luo,Fanrong Dong,Jiaping Wu,Chenxi Xiao,Wanlin Li,Ziyuan Jiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CAAT 提出了一种轻量级的接触感知框架，通过注意力缩放和动态触觉掩码显式地将接触先验融入 Transformer 策略中，在接触前强调视觉信息、接触期间强调触觉信息，并在仿真和真实实验中显著提升了多种策略架构的操作成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 Transformer 基线策略采用 token 拼接或可学习门控进行视觉-触觉融合，缺乏显式的接触感知先验，难以从演示中高效学习有效的跨模态表征，尤其在接触密集操作任务中表现不佳。

Method: 提出 CAAT（Contact-Aware Attention）框架：(1) 注意力缩放——根据接触状态动态调整视觉与触觉 token 的注意力权重，接触前强调视觉、接触中强调触觉；(2) 动态触觉掩码——将当前触觉观测与非接触参考帧比较，抑制静态背景 token；(3) 可无缝集成到主流 Transformer 策略（如 ACT、Diffusion Policy、π₀），无需修改动作解码器。

Result: 仿真中，CAAT + ACT 相比直接视觉-触觉融合提升 18.0 个百分点，相比门控融合提升 10.0 个百分点。真实世界实验中，基于视觉-触觉 UMI 平台，CAAT 在 ACT、Diffusion Policy 和 π₀ 上平均成功率达 60.0%，比最强基线平均高出 21.1 个百分点。

Conclusion: 显式接触先验与动态触觉掩码能有效改善视觉-触觉策略学习和跨架构的任务性能，CAAT 为接触密集操作提供了一种通用、轻量且高效的跨模态融合方案。

Abstract: In contact-rich manipulation, visual observations primarily guide motion in free space, whereas tactile observations become particularly informative during contact. However, standard Transformer-based visuo-tactile policies typically rely on either token concatenation or learnable gating. These approaches lack explicit contact-aware priors, making it difficult to efficiently learn effective cross-modal representations from demonstrations. To address this limitation, we propose CAAT, a lightweight contact-aware framework that explicitly incorporates contact priors through attention scaling and dynamic tactile masking. Specifically, CAAT emphasizes visual information before contact and tactile information during contact. It also suppresses static background tokens by comparing the current tactile observation with a non-contact reference. CAAT can be integrated into commonly used Transformer-based policies without modifying their action decoders. In simulation, integrating CAAT with ACT improves the average success rate by 18.0 percentage points over direct visuo-tactile fusion and by 10.0 percentage points over gated fusion. In real-world experiments using a visuo-tactile UMI platform, CAAT achieves an average success rate of 60.0% across ACT, Diffusion Policy, and $π_0$, outperforming the strongest baseline by an average of 21.1 percentage points. These results demonstrate that explicit contact priors and dynamic tactile masking are effective in improving visuo-tactile policy learning and task performance of diverse policy architectures. https://mrjiangjm.github.io/caat/

</details>


### [53] [FeDepth: Federated Learning for Depth Estimation under Robot Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01129)
*Ganghyeon Lee,Inha Lee,Junhee Lee,Jeongeon Lee,Sung Whan Yoon,Kyungdon Joo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FeDepth，一种基于描述符的软聚类联邦学习框架，用于解决机器人深度估计中客户端间数据分布重叠、域边界模糊的非IID问题，克服了传统硬聚类FL假设域可清晰分离的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人感知的联邦学习方法通常假设客户端可划分为清晰分离的域（硬聚类），但实际机器人部署中，不同平台、环境和感知条件下的数据分布高度重叠，域之间存在连续且模糊的过渡，导致传统聚类FL性能严重退化。该问题在深度估计领域尤其未被探索。

Method: 提出FeDepth框架：(1) 定义两种新的非IID场景，反映平台、环境和深度分布三个维度的异构性；(2) 采用基于描述符的软聚类策略建模客户端关系，允许每个客户端同时参与多个聚类，而非强制分配到单一簇，从而捕捉机器人环境中连续且模糊的域转换。

Result: 在多种深度估计架构上的大量实验表明，FeDepth在鲁棒性上一致优于标准联邦学习和硬聚类联邦学习基线。

Conclusion: FeDepth为联邦机器人感知提供了实用且有效的解决方案，软聚类机制能够更好地适应真实机器人部署中的数据分布重叠特性。

Abstract: Although recent robot perception research emphasizes training on data from diverse environments to improve generalization, most existing methods still rely on centralized learning, which is inefficient and difficult to scale across heterogeneous robot platforms. Federated learning (FL) offers an alternative by enabling distributed training without raw data transfer, but it suffers from severe performance degradation under domain shifts caused by heterogeneity across clients. In real robotic deployments, data distributions often overlap across platforms, environments, and sensing conditions, making it difficult to partition clients into clearly separated domains. However, this characteristic breaks the assumption of clearly separable client domains commonly used in clustered FL. To address this gap in robot perception, particularly in depth estimation, we introduce two realistic and unexplored non-IID scenarios that reflect heterogeneity in terms of platform, environment, and depth distribution. We then propose FeDepth, a descriptor-based clustered FL framework that models client relationships through soft clustering. Unlike hard clustering methods that assume clearly separated clusters, FeDepth allows clients to participate in multiple clusters, capturing continuous and ambiguous domain transitions commonly observed in robotic environments. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FeDepth consistently improves robustness over standard FL and clustered FL baselines across multiple depth estimation architectures, providing a practical and effective solution for federated robot perception. Our project page is available at https://vision3d-lab.github.io/fedepth/.

</details>


### [54] [Complete Motion Planning using Workspace-Fibered Decomposition for nR-Planar Manipulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01172)
*Aayush Rath,Antony Thomas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于工作空间纤维分解的框架，通过递归构造低维非冗余子链的障碍约束可达工作空间，并沿冗余姿态纤维增量提升，最终在降维的规划流形上进行运动规划，避免显式构建完整构型空间障碍几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在杂乱环境中为平面冗余机械臂进行运动规划时，直接在完整 n 维构型空间中处理障碍几何的计算成本极高。作者希望利用冗余运动学中工作空间可分解为低维非冗余子链可达区与冗余姿态纤维的层次结构，在保留分支一致性可达信息的前提下实现降维规划，并实现早期不可行性检测。

Method: 首先对平面纯位置机械臂，证明由最小非冗余子链诱导的障碍约束可达工作空间可精确表征相对于起始构型连通分支的可行性，从而在引入冗余自由度之前进行早期不可行性检测。随后提出增量纤维提升过程：利用雅可比行列式连续性约束保持局部逆运动学分支一致性，将可达工作空间结构通过逐级冗余连杆传播。最终在递归构造的工作空间-纤维流形上直接进行降维空间规划。

Result: 在冗余 nR 平面机械臂上的实验表明，所提构造方法在逐级提升过程中能够保碰撞自由连通性结构，同时相比直接在构型空间中推理，显著降低了碰撞检测复杂度。

Conclusion: 该框架利用工作空间纤维分解实现了冗余机械臂的高效运动规划，避免了全维构型空间障碍的显式构造，在保证连通性与分支一致性的前提下大幅降低了计算复杂度，为冗余机械臂在杂乱环境中的规划提供了有效新思路。

Abstract: We propose a workspace-fibered decomposition framework for motion planning in nR planar redundant manipulators operating in cluttered environments. Rather than planning directly in the full n-dimensional configuration space, the method incrementally constructs obstacle-constrained reachable workspaces of lower-dimensional non-redundant sub-chains and recursively lifts them through redundant orientation fibers. This yields a sequence of reduced planning manifolds that preserve branch-consistent reachability structure while avoiding explicit construction of the full configuration-space obstacle geometry. We first establish that, for planar position-only manipulators, the obstacle-constrained reachable workspace induced by the minimal non-redundant sub-chain provides an exact characterization of feasibility with respect to the connected component of the start configuration, enabling early infeasibility detection prior to introducing redundant degrees of freedom (DOF). We then introduce an incremental fiber-lifting procedure that propagates reachable workspace structure through successive redundant links while enforcing local inverse-kinematic branch consistency using Jacobian determinant continuity constraints. The resulting representation admits efficient reduced-space planning directly on recursively-constructed workspace-fiber manifolds. Experimental results on redundant nR planar manipulators demonstrate that the proposed construction preserves collision-free connectivity structure across successive lifting stages while substantially reducing collision checking complexity relative to direct configuration space reasoning.

</details>


### [55] [PRISM: Privileged Probabilistic Latent Supervision for End-to-End Autonomous Driving Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01201)
*Volodymyr Havrylov,Faris Janjoš,Andreas Look,Jürgen Mathes,Andreas Geiger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 端到端自动驾驶模型常因仅依赖输出监督而导致隐层梯度弱化。本文揭示近期VLM特征监督的增益实际源于与真值数据的隐式连接，而非VLM推理能力。基于此，作者提出概率深度监督框架，用ELBO直接正则化中间表示，在nuScenes上实现规划误差降低8%、碰撞率降低3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期工作将VLM监督引入端到端自动驾驶模型的隐层特征训练，获得显著性能提升，但其背后的理论机制尚不清晰。本文旨在揭示该方法的真实作用机理，并据此设计更直接有效的监督范式。

Method: 首先对VLM监督方法的机理进行深入分析，发现性能增益源于训练过程中E2E AD模型与真值(GT)数据之间建立的隐式连接，而非VLM的推理能力。基于此洞察，提出概率深度监督框架：将模型隐变量建模为可重参数化的分布，通过证据下界(ELBO)直接优化中间潜在表示与未来真值轨迹之间的关系。该方法完全移除VLM，直接用GT数据正则化轨迹相关隐变量。

Result: 在nuScenes数据集上，使用相同训练数据和E2E架构，相比竞争性向量化基线方法，规划L2误差降低8%，碰撞率降低3%，且计算开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: VLM监督在E2E AD中的有效性并非来自视觉语言推理，而是来自与GT的隐式对比学习效应。直接通过概率深度监督框架用GT数据正则化隐变量，可以更简洁高效地实现更优的规划性能，同时避免了VLM带来的计算和标注成本。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving (E2E AD) systems integrate perception, prediction, and planning into a single differentiable architecture. While these models show great promise, their standard training often relies on output-only supervision, which can lead to weak gradients for the hidden layers of increasingly complex models. Recent works have integrated vision-language model (VLM) supervision for latent features to address this, yielding substantial empirical gains, yet leaving the underlying theoretical mechanisms poorly understood. Our investigation into this methodology reveals that the resulting performance gains stem not from VLM reasoning capabilities, as previously assumed, but rather from the latent connections forged between the E2E AD model and ground-truth (GT) data during training. Building on this insight, we propose a probabilistic deep supervision framework that regularizes intermediate latent representations directly from GT data. By treating model latents as reparameterizable distributions, we optimize the architecture via the Evidence Lower Bound (ELBO). Our evaluations conducted on the nuScenes dataset demonstrate that supervising trajectory-related latents with future GT paths consistently improves planning performance. Using identical training data and E2E architectures, our method achieves an 8% reduction in planning L2 error and a 3% decrease in collision rates compared to competitive vectorized baselines, all while incurring negligible computational overhead.

</details>


### [56] [EndoWAM: A Grounded World-Action Model for Generalizable Endoscopic Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01221)
*Jinsong Lin,Zikang Pan,Wanhao Liu,Chi Kit Ng,Liangjing Shao,Zihang Yu,Ziyu Wang,Yin Wang,Jiaxi Wang,Jeremy Yuen-Chun Teoh,Zhiyong Xiong,Huxin Gao,Hongliang Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EndoWAM 是首个面向机器人内窥镜导航的世界动作模型，通过未来定位机制将视频世界模型的预测视觉动态与离散动作专家耦合，在三个解剖部位的内窥镜手术中实现了鲁棒、可泛化的实时控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型内窥镜导航策略仅从当前观测预测动作，缺乏对未来动态的显式建模，在组织变形、遮挡和视角变化下鲁棒性不足。世界动作模型虽有潜力，但受限于内窥镜数据稀缺、视角多样性不足、解剖结构可变形及推理延迟高，难以直接应用。

Method: 提出 EndoWAM，核心创新为"未来定位"（future grounding）：利用视频世界模型中间去噪特征预测未来观测中的任务相关目标区域，将轻量级扩散Transformer与离散动作专家通过共享预测表示耦合，在单次去噪过程中实现实时控制。同时构建了 EndoMotion 数据集，涵盖输尿管镜、食管镜和 ERCP 三种手术。

Result: EndoWAM 在所有基线方法和替代定位策略上持续领先，并在未见过的视角、环境和目标上展现出强零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: EndoWAM 作为一种预测性、目标定位的框架，为视觉受限的内窥镜环境中实现精确、可泛化的长程导航奠定了基础。

Abstract: Autonomous endoscopic navigation can reduce clinicians' operational burden, yet robust control remains challenging due to tissue deformation, transient occlusions, and rapidly changing viewpoints. Existing learning-based policies typically predict actions from current observations without explicitly modeling future dynamics, limiting their robustness and reliability in safety-critical settings. World Action Models (WAMs) offer a promising alternative by coupling predictive visual dynamics with action generation, but extending them to robotic endoscopy remains challenging due to limited training data, restricted viewpoint diversity, deformable anatomy, and high inference latency. We present EndoWAM, which is, to our knowledge, the first WAM for generalizable robotic endoscopic navigation. EndoWAM introduces future grounding, which predicts task-relevant target regions in future observations from intermediate denoising features of a video world model. Specifically, EndoWAM couples a lightweight diffusion transformer for future target-region prediction with a discrete action expert through a shared predictive representation. This design injects target-aware supervision into predictive dynamics modeling, improving robustness to visual degradation and viewpoint changes while enabling real-time control in a single denoising pass. We further introduce EndoMotion, a robotic endoscopic motion dataset spanning three anatomically distinct procedures: ureteroscopy, esophagoscopy, and endoscopic retrograde cholangiopancreatography (ERCP). EndoWAM consistently outperforms all baselines and alternative grounding strategies, while demonstrating strong zero-shot generalization to unseen viewpoints, environments, and targets. These results establish EndoWAM as a predictive, target-grounded framework for accurate, generalizable, and long-horizon navigation in visually constrained endoscopic environments.

</details>


### [57] [Hermite Curves as Trajectory Priors for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01265)
*Qi Lv,Jianming Xing,Zhao Yang,Mingyuan Yao,Yinan Shi,Yawei Jueluo,Mike Zheng Shou,Xiang Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对VLA模型中动作块（action chunk）缺乏结构化导致运动不平滑和边界不连续的问题，本文引入Hermite轨迹先验，将动作块参数化为分段三次Hermite曲线，并通过三种变体实现。实验表明，Hermite Regularization作为辅助训练目标效果最优，在多个基准和真实机器人任务上显著提升成功率，且无推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型中的动作块（action chunk）通常展平为逐时间步的控制信号，缺乏显式结构先验，导致物理执行时出现锯齿状运动轨迹和块间边界不连续。需要一种结构化接口来显式施加平滑性和连续性约束。

Method: 将动作块轨迹参数化为分段三次Hermite曲线，由端点位置和速度定义。提出三种实现变体：(1) Hermite Tokens：自回归预测量化边界变量；(2) Hermite Scaffold：将动作分解为基础支架加残差；(3) Hermite Regularization：将Hermite先验作为辅助训练正则项。覆盖离散自回归和连续生成两种范式。

Result: Hermite Regularization在三者中表现最优：LIBERO成功率从95.9%提升至98.7%，LIBERO-plus从85.7%提升至90.9%，四项真实机器人任务从63.4%提升至90.0%，且无额外推理开销。轨迹分析表明，结构化轨迹先验作为学习归纳偏置比作为运行时约束更有效。

Conclusion: 显式结构化轨迹先验（Hermite曲线）作为学习归纳偏置能有效提升VLA模型的操控性能，Hermite Regularization在保持零推理开销的同时实现了最优效果。建议优先采用辅助正则化方式而非推理时硬约束来利用结构先验。

Abstract: Despite recent progress in Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models for robotic manipulation, the action chunk remains a weakly structured interface. Existing work typically flatten each chunk into per-timestep controls, relying on implicit data learning that manifests as jagged motion and boundary discontinuities during physical execution. To address these limitations, we introduce Hermite trajectory priors, parameterizing the chunk trajectory as a piecewise cubic Hermite curve defined by endpoint positions and velocities to explicitly enforce smoothness and continuity. We instantiate this fixed operator across discrete autoregressive and continuous generative paradigms via three variants: (1) Hermite Tokens, which predict quantized boundary variables autoregressively; (2) Hermite Scaffold, which decomposes clean actions into a base scaffold and residuals; and (3) Hermite Regularization, which applies the prior strictly as an auxiliary training objective. Across simulation benchmarks and real-robot platforms, Hermite Regularization achieves superior performance among these three variants, improving π0.5 baseline success rates from 95.9% to 98.7% on LIBERO, 85.7% to 90.9% on LIBERO-plus, and 63.4% to 90.0% across four real-robot tasks without additional inference overhead. Trajectory analyses reveal that explicitly structuring trajectory priors serves most effectively as a learning inductive bias rather than a runtime constraint.

</details>


### [58] [DreamTrajectory: Trajectory-Guided Action Generation with World Model Alignment for Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01381)
*Zheng Yang,Wenjie Zhang,Xiangyu Chen,Wenxuan Song,Xianpeng Wang,Yihang Kang,Wen Chen,Lujia Wang,Renjing Xu,Xiaowen Chu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DreamTrajectory 是一种面向语言条件移动操控的轨迹引导框架，通过联合预测末端执行器轨迹与全身动作块来提供显式任务空间引导，并利用轻量轨迹世界模型进行测试时搜索-预测-评分，以缩小规划与执行之间的差距。在 MS-HAB 和真实世界任务上均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 策略在移动操控中存在两个核心局限：(1) 直接从观测映射到全身动作，缺乏显式的任务空间运动规划，导致基座-手臂协调预测不精确；(2) 开环执行预测的动作块，未验证动作是否能实现预期运动，导致控制误差与未建模接触累积，形成规划与实现之间的偏差。

Method: 提出 DreamTrajectory 框架，包含两个关键组件：(1) 单一动作专家中联合预测意图级末端执行器轨迹与全身动作块，使轨迹显式引导基座-手臂动作生成；(2) 构建轻量轨迹世界模型，预测候选动作块将引发的轨迹，并通过测试时搜索-预测-评分（search-predict-score）过程选择与规划轨迹最一致的候选动作。

Result: 在 MS-HAB 基准上，轨迹引导将平均成功率从 32.3% 提升至 47.5%，测试时优化进一步提至 54.8%，其中接触丰富的关节物体任务增益最大。在三个真实世界移动操控任务中，对应平均成功率分别为 63.3%、81.7% 和 90.0%。

Conclusion: DreamTrajectory 通过引入显式轨迹引导和基于世界模型的测试时搜索，有效解决了 VLA 策略在移动操控中缺乏任务空间规划与开环执行偏差的两大问题，显著提升了协调性与任务成功率，尤其在接触丰富的场景中表现突出。

Abstract: Mobile manipulation requires a robot to coordinate base and arm motion under continuously changing viewpoints and contact conditions, within an action space far larger than that of fixed-base manipulation. Existing Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies are limited in two respects. (i)They map observations directly to whole-body action chunks, searching this large action space without an explicit task-space motion plan, which makes coordinated base--arm prediction imprecise. (ii)They execute the predicted chunk open-loop, without checking whether the actions can realize the motion the policy intended, so control errors and unmodeled contacts accumulate into a gap between planned and realized motion. We present DreamTrajectory, a trajectory-guided framework for language-conditioned mobile manipulation that introduces one component for each limitation. Addressing(i), DreamTrajectory jointly predicts an intention-level end-effector trajectory and a whole-body action chunk in a single action expert, so that the trajectory explicitly guides base--arm action generation instead of remaining implicit. Addressing(ii), a lightweight trajectory world model predicts the trajectory that a candidate action chunk would induce, and a test-time search--predict--score procedure selects the candidate best aligned with the planned trajectory. On MS-HAB, trajectory guidance raises average success from 32.3% to 47.5% and test-time refinement further to 54.8%, with the largest gains on contact-rich articulated-object tasks. On three real-world mobile manipulation tasks, the corresponding average success rates are 63.3%, 81.7%, and 90.0%.

</details>


### [59] [SG-WAM: Self-Guided World Modeling in Geometry-Aware Policy Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01397)
*Ruiteng Zhao,Zhengshen Zhang,Yue Su,Wenshuo Wang,Jiahui Li,Zhiyuan Yang,Francis E. H. Tay,Marcelo H. Ang,Haiyue Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SG-WAM 提出一种自引导的几何感知世界动作模型，直接在策略表征空间中学习动作条件的未来状态预测，通过联合优化潜在预测、几何监督与流匹配动作生成，在 LIBERO 等基准上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAM）的未来动态建模空间要么依赖感知上冗余的观测空间目标，要么使用未针对动作相关性与几何感知联合优化的辅助潜空间，无法同时满足与动作生成对齐和充分几何感知的要求。

Method: 提出 SG-WAM 自引导框架：(1) 引入可学习动态令牌与自引导世界预测器，在策略主干提取的表征空间中预测动作条件下的未来潜状态；(2) 使用策略主干的指数移动平均复制版生成预测目标，提供稳定监督；(3) 对策略图像令牌施加几何监督，构建动作相关且几何感知的未来对齐空间；(4) 将潜未来预测、几何接地与流匹配动作生成在统一框架中端到端联合优化，基于 0.9B 模型，无大规模具身预训练。

Result: SG-WAM 在 LIBERO 上取得 98.5% 平均成功率，在 LIBERO-Plus 上达 73%，在分布内和分布外真实世界评估中均优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 在策略表征空间中统一动作相关性与几何感知的未来建模，是构建高效世界动作模型的关键，SG-WAM 验证了该设计思路的有效性与泛化能力。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) couple action generation with prediction of future states. Their effectiveness depends on whether future dynamics are modeled in a space that is both aligned with action generation and sufficiently geometry-aware to capture where and how actions change the scene. Existing WAMs typically satisfy only part of this requirement, relying on either perceptually heavy observation-space targets or auxiliary latent spaces that are not jointly structured for action relevance and geometry. We propose SG-WAM, a self-guided framework that learns geometry-aware action-conditioned dynamics directly in the policy-derived representation space. SG-WAM introduces learnable dynamics tokens and a Self-Guided World Predictor that forecasts their future latent states conditioned on intervening robot actions. Prediction targets are generated by an exponential moving average copy of the same policy backbone, providing stable supervision within the representation family used by the action expert. Geometric supervision further structures the policy image-token representations, providing spatially grounded context for the dynamics tokens and yielding a future-alignment space that is both action-relevant and geometry-aware. Latent future prediction, geometric grounding, and flow-matching action generation are jointly optimized end-to-end in a unified framework. Built on a 0.9B model without large-scale embodied pretraining, SG-WAM achieves 98.5% average success on LIBERO and 73% on LIBERO-Plus, while outperforming strong baselines in both in-distribution and out-of-distribution real-world evaluations.

</details>


### [60] [Demystifying When and Why VLAs Fail in Contact-Rich Tasks and How to Fix Them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01402)
*Carlota Parés-Morlans,Nils Kuhn,Isabel Liu,Alberta Longhini,Jeannette Bohg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文分析了VLA模型在接触密集操作任务中的两类根本性失败模式（精度失败和力失败），并针对性地提出FACT方法，在近2500次真实世界测试中将平均成功率从最佳基线的41%提升至66%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多通过力增强架构或训练正则化器来处理接触失败，但缺乏对VLA模型在接触密集任务中失败根因的深入理解。本文旨在系统性地识别失败模式并探究其成因。

Method: 作者首先识别出两类失败模式：（1）精度失败源于流匹配策略训练中的失配问题；（2）力失败源于力信号独特的结构特性。针对每种模式分别提出定向解决机制，并整合为统一的FACT方法。

Result: 在五个接触密集操作任务、近2500次真实世界 rollout 评估中，FACT 取得66%的平均成功率，显著优于最佳先前基线方法的41%。

Conclusion: 通过识别并定向解决精度失败和力失败这两类根本性失败模式，FACT 有效弥合了VLA模型在接触密集操作任务中的性能差距，大幅提升了真实世界任务成功率。

Abstract: We address the problem of understanding when and why Vision-Language-Action models struggle with contact-rich manipulation tasks that require precise physical interaction. Prior work has primarily focused on addressing contact failures through force-augmented architectures and training-time regularizers, yet the root causes of these failures remain underexplored. We identify two distinct failure modes underlying this gap. Precision failures are rooted in a flow-matching policy training mismatch, and force failures arise from the distinctive structure of force signals. We address each failure mode with a targeted mechanism and combine them into FACT, which achieves 66% average success rate across five contact-rich tasks against 41% for the best prior baseline, in an evaluation spanning almost 2,500 real-world rollouts.

</details>


### [61] [GenTrack: Physical Alignment for Robot-Native Motion Generation and Zero-Shot Humanoid Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01410)
*Zeyu Ling,Xinyao Yu,Renye Yan,Jikang Cheng,Zhanke Wang,Qing Shuai,Changqing Zou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GenTrack 提出在线生成器-跟踪器联合训练框架，通过交替进行执行锚定的生成器对齐与跟踪器训练，缩小重定向运动与机器人可执行运动之间的差距，显著提升零样本人形机器人控制的覆盖范围与跟踪精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用人形跟踪器依赖昂贵的大规模具身语料库，而文本-运动生成器虽提供可扩展监督，但基于人类运动或重定向数据训练的模型存在运动学合理性与机器人可执行性之间的鸿沟。单向流水线无法同时优化生成器与跟踪器，限制了零样本覆盖能力。

Method: GenTrack 采用在线生成器-跟踪器交替训练框架：生成器通过执行锚定的群组相对对齐（execution-grounded, group-relative alignment）产生更适配机器人的运动；跟踪器在新生成的参照上训练；引入锚定（anchoring）与复述（rehearsal）机制约束漂移。在 Unitree G1 平台上，结合 ProtoMotions 和 SONIC 骨干网络进行验证。

Result: 在三个零样本跟踪分割任务（公开 AMASS、LAFAN 基准及 1024 组私有分布外 prompt-motion 测试集）上，在线联合训练策略持续使生成器输出更具机器人可执行性且语义对齐良好的运动，跟踪器获得显著更广的零样本覆盖与更高的跟踪精度，尤其在分布外参照上表现突出。

Conclusion: 联合在线后训练可有效弥合重定向参照与机器人原生运动之间的可执行性差距，在无需额外数据采集的前提下推进零样本人形控制，突破静态参照池的局限。

Abstract: General-purpose humanoid trackers can execute diverse references, but their zero-shot coverage depends on large embodied corpora that are costly to extend. Text-to-motion generators offer scalable supervision, yet models trained on human motion or retargeted data inherit a gap between kinematic plausibility and robot executability. Existing one-way pipelines fix either the generated corpus or the reward tracker. We introduce GenTrack, an online generator--tracker framework that alternates execution-grounded, group-relative generator alignment with tracker training on newly generated references; anchoring and rehearsal constrain drift. On Unitree G1, we evaluate GenTrack with ProtoMotions and SONIC backbones across three zero-shot tracking splits including public AMASS and LAFAN benchmarks, and a private out-of-distribution test set of 1,024 prompt-motion pairs in the wild. The online co-training strategy consistently produces generators that output more robot-executable motions with strong semantic alignment, and trackers with markedly broader zero-shot coverage and improved tracking accuracy, especially on out-of-distribution references. These results demonstrate that joint online post-training effectively narrows the executability gap between retargeted references and robot-native motion, advancing zero-shot humanoid control without additional data collection and beyond the limitations of a static reference pool.

</details>


### [62] [When Replanning Becomes the Bottleneck: Budgeted Replanning for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01428)
*Shuaijun Liu,Feiyang You,Xingwei Chen,Ningxin Su*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BRACE 是一个将具身智能体的重规划建模为预算控制回路的控制器，结合成本感知的渐进式 token 剪枝方法 E-RECAP，大幅减少重规划调用的 token 数量和延迟违反率，同时保持或提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体频繁重规划时，累积的文本上下文不断增长，导致基于 LLM 的重规划延迟出现重尾分布，可能错过实时截止时间。这种失败模式难以通过平均延迟或成功率检测，需要一种面向尾部延迟的预算控制方法。

Method: 提出 BRACE 控制器，将重规划形式化为预算控制回路，决定是否重规划、选择重规划模式、分配显式 token 预算和延迟服务等级目标（SLO），并集成可选效率模块。同时提出 E-RECAP，一种成本感知的渐进式 token 剪枝方法，在 transformer 各层预测 token 效用并剪枝上下文，保留关键的头尾 token。

Result: 在 Meta Habitat、RoboFactory 和 AirSim 上，BRACE + E-RECAP 将重规划调用的 token 数减少 62-92%，SLO 违反率从 85.5-100.0% 降至 4.7-50.0%。在更困难的 RoboFactory 设定中，开环、冻结计划和 No BRACE 均失败，而 BRACE + E-RECAP 达到 80.0% 成功率且仅有 4.6% SLO 违反。

Conclusion: 面向尾部的逐调用预算控制在多种具身平台上是有效的，BRACE 和 E-RECAP 的组合可在保持高任务成功率的同时，显著降低 token 消耗和实时延迟违反。

Abstract: Embodied agents replan frequently to recover from execution drift, partial observability, and coordination hazards, but each LLM-based replanning call can consume an accumulated textual context that grows over time and across agents. Once this context becomes large, replanning latency develops heavy tails and can miss real-time deadlines even when task success remains high, a failure mode that is hard to detect from average latency or success alone. We present BRACE, a controller that formulates replanning as a budgeted control loop by deciding whether to replan, selecting a replanning mode, and allocating an explicit token budget and latency service-level objective (SLO) while accounting for optional efficiency modules. As a reusable component, we introduce E-RECAP, a cost-aware progressive token pruning method that predicts token utility and prunes replanning contexts across transformer layers while preserving critical head and tail tokens. Across Meta Habitat, RoboFactory, and AirSim, BRACE with E-RECAP reduces replanning-call token counts by 62-92% and SLO violation rates from 85.5-100.0% to 4.7-50.0% in settings where task success is already saturated. In a harder RoboFactory setting where open-loop, frozen-plan, and No BRACE all fail, BRACE + E-RECAP reaches 80.0% success with 4.6% SLO violations, demonstrating that tail-aware per-call budgeting is effective across embodied platforms.

</details>


### [63] [DynamicManip: Enabling Dynamic Manipulation from a Single Static Demonstration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01452)
*Haoran Liao,Pengyue Wang,Shuoyu Chen,Kehan Cheng,Xuhang Chen,Yuhao Lin,Mu Lin,Zhizhao Liang,Xiaoyi Fan,Chengyi Xing,Dan Niu,Yi-Lin Wei,Wei-Shi Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DynamicManip 提出了一个面向机器人动态操控的高效数据增强管道和低延迟模仿策略，通过静态到动态的演示合成与动态感知的自适应推理频率，在数据效率和任务成功率上均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人动态操控面临两大核心挑战：一是动态场景的组合复杂性导致数据需求巨大，二是动态快速变化要求策略具备实时且精确的执行能力。现有方法难以同时解决数据效率低和推理延迟高的问题。

Method: （1）静态到动态数据增强管道：从单条静态演示出发，合成多样化的动态操控演示数据，大幅降低数据采集成本；（2）动态感知自适应策略：根据任务动态特性自适应调整推理频率，实现低延迟响应；（3）构建动态操控基准测试集，包含多种动态任务及自动化评估系统。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界实验中，DynamicManip 相比基线方法平均成功率提升 18.4 个百分点，策略查询延迟降低 32.9%，在数据效率和动态操控性能上均表现出显著优势。

Conclusion: DynamicManip 通过数据增强和自适应策略有效解决了动态操控中数据效率低和实时性差的问题，为机器人在复杂动态环境中的操控能力提供了实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Dynamic manipulation is a critical capability for robots operating in complex and dynamic environments, where robots must interact with objects that are moving or require rapid adjustments. However, learning models for dynamic manipulation tasks face two major challenges: (1) the combinatorial complexity of dynamic scenarios leads to substantial data requirements, and (2) rapid variations in dynamics require real-time and accurate policy execution. In this paper, we propose DynamicManip to address these challenges through an efficient data augmentation pipeline and a low-latency imitation policy. We first propose a static-to-dynamic augmentation pipeline that synthesizes diverse dynamic manipulation demonstrations from a single static demonstration. Second, we introduce a dynamic-aware adaptive policy that adjusts its inference frequency according to task dynamics, enabling responsive and effective dynamic manipulation. Third, we build a dynamic manipulation benchmark, which includes diverse dynamic tasks with an automatic evaluation system for scalable and consistent assessment. Extensive experiments in both simulation and the real world demonstrate that DynamicManip not only provides significant improvements in data efficiency but also achieves better performance in dynamic manipulation tasks, with a mean success rate 18.4 percentage points higher and policy-query latency 32.9% lower.

</details>


### [64] [Rapid Embodiment Adaptation for Quadrupedal Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01506)
*Dichen Li,Bo Ai,Nico Bohlinger,Jan Peters,Hao Su,Henrik I. Christensen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种在线具身适应框架，通过短时交互历史推断四足机器人的具身参数（关节约束和躯干质量变化），并以此调节控制策略，实现快速适应（半秒内）。在仿真和真实Go2机器人上验证，能处理锁死腿和5kg负载等严重退化场景，显著优于非自适应方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能随身体变化（老化、受伤、负重）自然调整运动，但基于学习的机器人策略在硬件属性变化时往往失效。现有方法直接依赖交互历史隐式适应，缺乏对具身参数变化的显式建模，导致适应速度慢、鲁棒性差。

Method: 提出在线具身适应框架，核心包括两部分：(1) 在具身随机化下训练的通才策略（generalist policy），(2) 轻量级适应模块，从短时交互历史（约0.5秒）中显式推断具身参数变化，并将推断的硬件状态作为控制条件输入策略。重点评估两种具身变化：关节运动范围约束（模拟运动学退化）和躯干质量变化（模拟动力学变化）。

Result: 仿真中，适应模块能准确估计关节约束和质量变化，闭环控制性能大幅优于直接以交互历史为条件的策略。真实Unitree Go2机器人上，系统在严重退化场景下保持稳定运动，包括单腿完全锁死和额外负载5kg，而非自适应方法均失败。

Conclusion: 显式在线具身识别方法能实现对关节极限和负载质量变化的快速适应，具有实用性，为处理更广泛的不确定、退化或变化的机器人硬件状态迈出了重要一步。

Abstract: Humans readily adapt their movements as their bodies change through aging, injury, or load carrying, but learning-based robot policies often break when hardware properties shift. We introduce an online embodiment adaptation framework for quadrupedal locomotion that infers embodiment parameters from short interaction histories and conditions control on the inferred hardware state. Our method pairs a generalist policy trained under embodiment randomization with a lightweight adaptation module that identifies physical changes within half a second. We evaluate two representative forms of embodiment variation: joint-range constraints and trunk-mass changes, corresponding to joint-level kinematic degradation and body-level dynamic variation. In simulation, the module accurately estimates these changes and enables closed-loop control that substantially outperforms policies conditioned directly on interaction history. On a real Unitree Go2 robot, our system maintains stable locomotion under severe instances of the evaluated changes, including a fully locked leg and a 5 kg payload, where non-adaptive methods fail. These results demonstrate the practicality of explicit online embodiment identification for rapid adaptation to joint-limit and payload-mass changes, and provide a step toward handling broader forms of uncertain, degraded, or changing robot hardware.

</details>


### [65] [Learning Smooth SE(3) Trajectories under Left-Invariant Riemannian Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01562)
*Yuwei Wu,Vijay Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于学习的框架，用于在SE(3)上的一般左不变黎曼度量下近似生成高阶光滑轨迹。该方法用神经网络学习部分多项式系数和轨迹时长，其余系数由边界条件解析确定，训练由欧拉-拉格朗日最优性条件引导，在毫秒级推理时间内逼近数值优化解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SE(3)上刚体运动轨迹生成的变分方法在任意边界状态和耦合旋转-平移度量下缺乏闭式解，依赖计算昂贵的数值边界值求解器，限制了其在实时机器人规划与控制中的应用。需要一个高效且几何一致的替代方案。

Method: 采用学习框架逼近SE(3)上一般左不变黎曼度量下的高阶光滑轨迹：(1)用高阶多项式参数化体旋量轨迹；(2)神经网络学习部分多项式系数和轨迹时长，其余系数由边界条件解析确定；(3)训练损失由欧拉-拉格朗日最优性条件、度量加权光滑性目标和可行性约束构成；(4)度量条件化使框架能泛化到不同度量结构和运动条件。

Result: 大量数值实验表明，该方法生成的轨迹在质量上逼近数值优化解，且推理时间仅为毫秒级。在两类实际应用中验证了其有效性：带航点遍历的多样化运动基元实时生成，以及动态条件下的四旋翼飞行轨迹细化。

Conclusion: 具有几何结构的学习运动生成方法可成为SE(3)上轨迹生成中传统优化方法的有效替代方案，兼顾了求解质量与实时性需求。

Abstract: Optimal trajectory generation for rigid-body motions on Lie groups can be formulated as a variational problem that minimizes energy functionals defined by Riemannian metrics. While closed-form solutions exist for special cases such as product metrics and rest-to-rest boundary conditions, solving the general problem with arbitrary boundary states and coupled rotational-translational metrics often requires computationally expensive numerical boundary value solvers. These limitations restrict the use of geometrically consistent trajectory generation in real-time robotic planning and control. This paper presents a learning-based framework for approximating higher-order smooth trajectories on SE(3) under general left-invariant Riemannian metrics. The method parameterizes body-twist trajectories using high-order polynomials and relies on a neural network to learn a subset of the polynomial coefficients and the trajectory duration. The remaining coefficients are analytically determined to enforce the boundary conditions. The training of the network is guided by losses derived from Euler-Lagrange optimality conditions, metric-weighted smoothness objectives, and feasibility constraints. The metric-conditioned framework enables generalization across diverse metric structures and motion conditions. Extensive numerical experiments demonstrate that the proposed approach generates smooth trajectories that closely approximate solutions from numerical optimization while achieving millisecond-level inference times. We demonstrate two practical applications of the proposed framework: real-time generation of diverse motion primitives with waypoint traversal, and refinement for quadrotor flight under dynamic conditions. These results suggest that learning-based motions with geometric structure can provide an efficient alternative to conventional optimization-based methods for trajectory generation on SE(3).

</details>


### [66] [Uncovering and Mitigating Positional Blind Spots in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01573)
*Dongdong An,Pengjie Zhao,Yihao Huang,Wenbing Tang,Ziming He,Jiayi Zhu,Jifeng Ning,Qin Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLA模型在机器人操作中存在"位置盲区"（Positional Blind Spots）——即任务无关干扰物的位置变动会显著提高局部区域的失败率。本文提出一个两阶段黑盒框架：先通过网格化workspace和对数似然比检验发现PBS，再用LoRA在这些区域微调策略，最终将整体失败率降低40%–85.19%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型的评估隐含假设整个workspace内能力均匀，但实际上，即使指令和场景其他因素不变，仅移动任务无关的干扰物就会在某些局部空间区域导致失败率急剧上升。这种"位置盲区"现象尚未被系统研究和解决。

Method: 提出两阶段黑盒框架：(1) 发现阶段：将workspace网格化，采用单侧对数似然比检验（one-sided log-likelihood-ratio test）定位失败风险显著偏高的PBS单元格；(2) 缓解阶段：在发现的PBS区域收集演示数据，通过LoRA对VLA策略进行针对性微调，在不损害其他区域表现的前提下提升PBS区域的能力。

Result: 在5个SOTA VLA策略和2个基准上验证：(1) PBS普遍存在且空间集中，失败率最高达0.58；(2) 搜索策略F1-score平均0.678，分别比随机搜索和自适应采样基线高0.268和0.178；(3) 基于发现区域的定向微调将整体失败率降低40.00%–85.19%。

Conclusion: 位置盲区是VLA模型中普遍且被忽视的脆弱性，本文提出的发现-缓解框架能够有效定位并修复这些盲区，显著提升策略的鲁棒性，且方法具有黑盒通用性，适用于多种现有VLA策略。

Abstract: Recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models achieve promising performance in robotic manipulation, typically measured by success rates aggregated over predefined object configurations, an evaluation that implicitly assumes spatially uniform competence across the workspace. However, this assumption does not hold: even with the instruction and every other scene factor held fixed, merely relocating a task-irrelevant distractor can sharply raise the failure probability within localized, spatially coherent regions, which we term Positional Blind Spots (PBS). In this paper, we propose a two-stage black-box framework to uncover and mitigate PBS. During the uncovering stage, we grid the workspace and apply a one-sided log-likelihood-ratio test to localize PBS cells with significantly elevated risk. During the mitigation stage, we fine-tune the policy via LoRA on demonstrations collected from these PBS regions, improving competence there while largely preserving performance across the rest of the workspace. We evaluate our framework on five state-of-the-art VLA policies across two benchmarks, and find that PBS are pervasive and spatially concentrated in all of them, with failure rates up to 0.58. Our search strategy achieves an average F1-score of 0.678, outperforming random search and adaptive sampling baselines by 0.268 and 0.178, respectively. Guided by the discovered regions, targeted fine-tuning reduces the overall failure rate by 40.00%--85.19%.

</details>


### [67] [Perception-and-action system for humanoid robot task execution in construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01600)
*Yanxi Liu,Yizhi Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种感知-动作系统，包含Humanoid-PoseNet和Humanoid-ActionNet两个深度网络，使仿人机器人能够从工人演示中学习并执行建筑施工任务，实现了8种施工动作，平均关节位置误差82.45 mm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿人机器人因其类人外形和多任务能力，非常适合土木与建筑工程等以人为主的工作场所，但现有研究鲜少赋予机器人执行实际施工任务的实用能力。因此，需要一种使仿人机器人从工人演示中学习施工技能的方法。

Method: 提出感知-动作系统，包含两个深度网络：(1) Humanoid-PoseNet，提取人体姿态并将其转换为仿人机器人机械可行的姿态；(2) Humanoid-ActionNet，基于转换后的姿态学习机器人可执行的动作。

Result: 仿人机器人可靠执行了8种施工相关动作，平均运动跟踪误差（MPJPE）为82.45 mm。

Conclusion: 该研究为在建筑施工场景中部署仿人机器人协作迈出了早期一步，验证了从工人演示中学习施工任务的可行性。

Abstract: Humanoid robots, with their human-like shape and multi-tasking capabilities, are well-aligned with human-dominated workplaces, like those in civil and construction engineering, where they could collaborate with human workers or autonomously perform physically demanding and hazardous tasks. Despite this promise, limited research has explored how to endow these robots with the practical capabilities needed to perform construction tasks. To this end, this study proposes a novel perception-and-action system that enables humanoid robots to learn and perform construction tasks from worker demonstrations. This system contains two deep networks: Humanoid-PoseNet, which extracts human postures and translates them into mechanically feasible poses for a humanoid robot; and Humanoid-ActionNet, which learns robot-executable actions based on these translated poses. Experimental results demonstrate that the humanoid robot reliably executed eight construction-related actions, achieving an average motion-tracking error of 82.45 mm MPJPE (Mean Per Joint Position Error). This work provides an early step toward deploying humanoid collaborators in construction.

</details>


### [68] [AffordTrajDP: Dynamic Affordance-Guided Visuomotor Policy Learning for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01603)
*Gaoyuan Wu,Ziyu Shan,Haoyang Du,Yuyao Jiang,Ziwei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AffordTrajDP 提出了一种动态框架，利用以物体为中心的时序传播构建affordance轨迹，取代传统静态affordance，解决精密操作中因物体位置扰动导致的后接触轨迹漂移问题，在仿真和真实实验中均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有affordance引导的模仿学习方法通常采用静态几何约束（如固定接触点），但在精度关键任务或物体位置发生扰动时，这种静态affordance会出现不一致，导致接触后轨迹漂移，影响操作成功率。

Method: 提出AffordTrajDP框架：首先从RGB-D观测中检索一个锚点affordance，捕捉末端执行器与目标物体之间的期望接触点；然后利用物体的SE(3)姿态作为传播介质，通过affordance propagation将锚点affordance向前传播，生成一条具备时序一致性和状态感知能力的affordance轨迹，为整个操作过程提供渐进式引导。

Result: 在ManiSkill3基准上达到70.0%的平均成功率，比强基线方法最高提升17.8%。在Galaxea A1和UR7e机械臂上进行了真实实验，涵盖StackCube、PickCup、AdapterInsertion、Ring-on-Peg、Put-in-Bowl和USB Insertion六类任务，验证了在物体放置变化和外观变化下的鲁棒性，并在Galaxea A1上评估了已知和未知物体实例。

Conclusion: 动态affordance轨迹能够有效克服静态affordance在精密操作中的局限性，通过物体SE(3)姿态的时序传播实现状态感知的持续引导，消融实验也证实了各组件的作用。

Abstract: Affordance-guided imitation learning has shown impressive performance in robotic manipulation tasks by compressing visual perception into task-specific geometric constraints (e.g., fixed contact points). However, the commonly used static affordances can become inconsistent in precision-critical tasks or under object location perturbations, leading to post-contact trajectory drift. To address this issue, we propose AffordTrajDP, a dynamic framework that constructs affordance trajectories via object-centric temporal propagation to guide the progressive manipulation process. Specifically, given an RGB-D observation, our core insight is that a retrieved anchor affordance, which captures the desired contact point between the end-effector and the target object, can be propagated forward via affordance propagation, using the object's SE(3) pose as a natural propagation medium, to yield an affordance trajectory that provides temporally consistent, state-aware guidance throughout execution. AffordTrajDP achieves 70.0% average success rate on ManiSkill3, outperforming strong baselines by up to 17.8%. Real-world experiments on Galaxea A1 and UR7e robotic arms, covering StackCube, PickCup, AdapterInsertion, Ring-on-Peg, Put-in-Bowl, and USB Insertion, further validate robustness under object placement variations and appearance changes, with seen and unseen object instances evaluated on Galaxea A1, and ablations confirm the contribution of each proposed component.

</details>


### [69] [A Forward-Inverse Dynamic Game Framework for Enhanced Multi-Agent Trajectory Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01636)
*Tianle Liu,Youcheng Niu,Jing Zeng,Shuo Li,Jinming Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种KL正则化动态博弈框架，用于非线性多智能体系统中的反馈纳什均衡求解。该方法通过状态依赖的自适应权重平衡最优性与行为先验，并结合基于最大熵逆强化学习的逆博弈模块推断未知成本参数，在合作导航与合并场景中得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态博弈方法通常假设智能体完全理性且目标已知，或依赖固定正则化，无法有效刻画有限理性以及安全关键场景中随空间变化的交互强度。

Method: 提出KL正则化动态博弈，引入状态依赖权重自适应平衡最优性与行为先验；开发基于最大熵逆强化学习与物理信息正则化的上下文感知逆博弈模块，用于从示教行为中推断未知成本参数；证明了正则化局部博弈的逐迭代适定性及自适应权重函数在名义轨迹有界更新下的Lipschitz连续性。

Result: 数值仿真与多机器人实验（合作导航与合并场景）验证了所提框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提框架能够有效建模有限理性与空间变化交互强度，实现了未知目标推断与自适应轨迹规划的统一，在多智能体安全关键应用中具有良好前景。

Abstract: This paper studies feedback Nash equilibrium (FBNE) seeking for multi-agent trajectory planning in nonlinear dynamical systems with unknown agents' objectives and state-dependent inter-agent coupling. While dynamic game theory provides a principled framework for such problems, existing approaches typically assume fully rational agents with known objectives or rely on fixed regularization, limiting their ability to capture bounded rationality and spatially varying interaction intensity in safety-critical settings. To this end, we propose a KL-regularized dynamic game with a state-dependent weight that adaptively balances optimality and behavioral priors. To infer unknown cost parameters from demonstrated behaviors, we develop a context-aware inverse game module based on maximum-entropy inverse reinforcement learning with physics-informed regularization, ensuring structural consistency with the forward game. We establish per-iteration well-posedness of the regularized local game and show that the adaptive weighting function remains Lipschitz continuous under bounded nominal-trajectory updates. Numerical simulations and multi-robot experiments on cooperative navigation and merging scenarios validate the effectiveness of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [70] [SyncPlan: Long-Horizon LLM Coordination with Explicit Synchronization and Adaptive Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01652)
*Shen You,Xiaoming Zhu,Weining Weng,Hefei Mei,Weixuan Wang,Zhongshen Li,Zeji LI,Ye-Wen Wang,Zijun Liao,Juchao Zhuo,Yang Wei,Fuhao Qiu,Siqin Li,Zhenjie Lian,Danei Gong,Junkai Ji,Xiangtao Li,Qiuzhen Lin,Liang Wang,Ka-Chun Wong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SyncPlan 是一种面向 LLM 多智能体协调的"计划-执行-修正"框架，通过显式同步原语和自适应修正机制，在单次 LLM 调用中生成协调计划，并在执行阶段通过等待原语、死锁检测和计划陈旧度检测器实现高效自适应协调，在 Overcooked 和 Honor of Kings 环境中以不足 0.05% 的实际运行时间取得 SOTA 成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体 LLM 协调方法面临效率与自适应性的根本权衡：反复调用 LLM 或多轮通信带来显著延迟，且易受异步进度和环境变化影响；而一次性规划虽减少协调开销，但产生的开环计划会迅速过时，尤其当动作依赖其他智能体和环境时。因此需要一种既能保持高效又能自适应环境变化的协调框架。

Method: 提出 SyncPlan 框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 集中式 LLM 协调器，在单次规划调用中为每个智能体生成动作链；(2) 执行阶段引入显式等待原语（wait primitives）和死锁检测机制，强制执行智能体间及智能体-环境依赖；(3) 轻量级计划陈旧度检测器（Plan Staleness Detector），持续评估剩余计划的有效性，当环境变化使计划假设失效时触发重规划。此外，通过监督微调（SFT）和面向规划的强化学习（RL）对协调器进行优化，RL 使用密集任务进度和结果级执行反馈作为奖励信号。

Result: 在公开的 Overcooked 基准和复杂的 Honor of Kings 环境中，SyncPlan 取得了最先进的任务成功率，同时实际运行时间（wall-clock runtime）不到现有基于 LLM 的协调器的 0.05%。

Conclusion: SyncPlan 通过将一次性集中规划与执行阶段的显式同步和自适应修正相结合，成功解决了 LLM 多智能体协调中效率与自适应性的权衡问题，在复杂长时域协调任务上兼具高性能与极低延迟，展示了"计划-执行-修正"范式的有效性。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent coordination faces a fundamental trade-off between efficiency and adaptivity in dynamic environments. Existing approaches typically rely on repeated LLM invocations or multi-round communication to adapt decisions during execution, introducing substantial latency and making coordination vulnerable to asynchronous progress and environmental changes. Conversely, one-shot planning reduces coordination overhead but produces open-loop plans that can quickly become stale or fail when actions depend on other agents and the environment.
