We introduce SyncPlan, a plan-execute-correct framework for long-horizon coordination through explicit synchronization and adaptive correction. Given the state and team-level task, a centralized LLM coordinator generates per-agent action chains in a single planning call. During execution, explicit wait primitives and deadlock detection enforce inter-agent and agent-environment dependencies, while a lightweight Plan Staleness Detector continuously assesses the remaining plan and triggers replanning when environmental changes invalidate its assumptions. We further optimize the coordinator through SFT and planning-oriented RL with dense task progress and outcome-level execution feedback. Experiments on the public Overcooked benchmark and the complex Honor of Kings environment show that SyncPlan achieves state-of-the-art task success rates while using less than 0.05% of the wall-clock runtime compared with existing LLM-based coordinators. Code and datasets will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [71] [ProtoAct: Turning Wet-Lab Protocols into Embodied Robotic Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01690)
*Zhe Liu,Jiaming Gu,Zhaohui Du,Zhe Wang,Huanbo Jin,Quan Lu,Qi Wang,Ting Xiao,Minting Pan,Dongzhan Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ProtoAct 是一个将自由格式生物实验协议转化为机器人可执行动作序列的结构化框架，通过检索增强解析、自省式检查与模式约束三个互补模块，在 BioP2E 数据集上验证了多 LLM 主干的可行性，并成功实现仿真与真实机器人执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物湿实验协议面向训练有素的研究人员编写，大量省略常规操作、状态依赖条件和上下文参数，导致难以被机器人直接翻译执行。亟需一种能够将自由形式文本转化为状态感知、可具身化动作序列的方法。

Method: 提出 ProtoAct 框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) ProtoRAG——检索人工标注范例进行上下文敏感解析；(2) RefineChecker——检测并修正缺失或不一致的步骤；(3) ActSchema——将精炼后的流程映射为受约束的 JSON 函数序列。同时构建 BioP2E 数据集，人工标注 22 个细胞培养协议，含 258 个监测条件、910 个可执行子任务和 962 个具身动作调用。

Result: 在七种大语言模型上评估，ProtoAct 可有效适配不同主干网络。消融实验证实检索、后验检查与模式约束三者贡献互补。解析后的子任务进一步支持演示收集和 VLA 模型训练，在仿真与真实机器人场景中均成功执行。

Conclusion: ProtoAct 在生物协议理解与具身机器人执行之间搭建了实用桥梁，为自动化生物实验提供了从文本到动作的可行接口。

Abstract: Biological wet-lab protocols are written for trained researchers and often leave routine operations, state-dependent conditions, and contextual parameters implicit, making them difficult to translate into robot-executable actions. We present ProtoAct, a structured protocol-grounding framework that converts free-form biological procedures into state-aware, embodiment-ready action sequences. ProtoAct uses ProtoRAG to retrieve manually annotated examples for context-sensitive parsing, employs RefineChecker to detect and revise missing or inconsistent steps, and applies ActSchema to map the refined procedure into constrained JSON function sequences. We further introduce BioP2E, for which we manually annotate 22 cell-culture protocols into 258 monitoring conditions, 910 executable subtasks, and 962 grounded action calls. Evaluation across seven large language models demonstrates that ProtoAct can be effectively instantiated with different backbones. Ablations confirm that retrieval, posterior checking, and schema constraints make complementary contributions. The parsed subtasks further support demonstration collection and VLA model training, enabling successful execution in both simulation and real-robot settings. ProtoAct thus provides a practical interface between biological protocol understanding and embodied robotic execution.

</details>


### [72] [Bridging the Sim-to-Real Gap in Parallel-Link Leg Mechanisms via Simulator-Side Dynamics Normalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01697)
*Jinsong Hong,Jangho Kim,Jihwan Lee,Donghyun Kim,Sehoon Oh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出S3N方法，通过在仿真器端对串行树替代模型的动力学进行归一化，补偿因串行化简化导致的执行器惯量、阻尼及连杆惯量差异，显著缩小了并联机构在sim-to-real中的动力学差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并联连杆机构在仿真中常被简化为串行树模型，传统基于雅可比的状态与力矩映射虽能保持运动学一致性，但无法处理因坐标变换引入的执行器惯量/阻尼再分配以及连杆惯量丢失问题，导致显著的sim-to-real动力学差距。

Method: 提出Simulator-Side System Normalization (S3N)，包含两个变体：(1) S3N-Act，通过坐标变换将执行器惯量和阻尼纳入串行坐标动力学；(2) S3N-Full，在S3N-Act基础上进一步通过分别辨识执行器级和腿级频率响应来恢复残余连杆惯量。该方法在保持串行树拓扑的前提下归一化仿真器有效动力学。

Result: 在2自由度平台上验证，S3N-Full相比雅可比映射基线，关节位置RMSE降低80.9%，力矩RMSE降低82.1%；俯仰原地运动中，S3N-Act和S3N-Full分别将地面反力范数RMSE降低65.1%和62.4%；圆周运动中，S3N-Full将相位平均、指令归一化的sim-to-real差距从17.3%降至9.9%。

Conclusion: 仿真器端归一化能有效提升运动和力层面的sim-to-real一致性，使基于串行树框架的策略训练具备与物理并联机构更一致的硬件动力学特性。

Abstract: This paper addresses the sim-to-real gap in dynamics arising when a parallel-link mechanism is represented by a serial-tree surrogate in simulation. Conventional Jacobian-based state and torque mappings preserve consistency with the kinematic and virtual-work relations but do not account for the coordinate-induced redistribution of actuator inertia and damping and the linkage inertia omitted during serial-tree reduction. To address this gap, Simulator-Side System Normalization (S3N) is proposed to normalize the serial-tree simulator's effective dynamics while preserving its tree topology. S3N-Act incorporates actuator inertia and damping into the serial-coordinate dynamics through coordinate transformation, whereas S3N-Full restores residual linkage inertia by separately identifying actuator- and leg-level frequency responses. In the 2-DoF validation, S3N-Full reduced the joint-position and torque RMSEs by 80.9% and 82.1%, respectively, relative to the Jacobian-mapping baseline. During pitch-in-place motion, S3N-Act and S3N-Full reduced the RMSE of the ground reaction force norm by 65.1% and 62.4%, respectively. During circular locomotion, S3N-Full reduced the phase-averaged, command-normalized sim-to-real gap from 17.3% to 9.9%. These results show that simulator-side normalization improves motion- and force-level sim-to-real consistency. It enables policy training in a serial-tree framework with hardware-consistent dynamics that better represent the physical parallel-link mechanism.

</details>


### [73] [TWINS: A Tactile Wearable Isomorphic Arm Networked System for Contact-Rich Manipulation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01733)
*Takahide Kitamura,Masaki Murooka,Natsuki Yamanobe,Yukiyasu Domae*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TWINS 是一种面向体表接触操作的机器人系统，由可穿戴式双臂示教装置和同构机器人组成，通过胸部和手臂的分布式触觉传感器实现体表接触感知，支持演示采集、模仿学习与自主执行的一体化流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作系统主要关注末端执行器操作，难以处理涉及手臂和胸部等身体表面接触的操控任务（如怀抱、推挤等），而此类任务在实际应用中非常重要，因此需要一套能够统一支持演示、学习和执行的体表接触操控系统。

Method: 提出 TWINS 系统，包含两部分：(1) 穿戴式双臂装置（Wearable Dual-Arm Device），由操作员穿戴，胸部和手臂嵌入分布式触觉传感器，可同步记录关节运动与体表接触数据；(2) 同构机器人（Isomorphic Robot），具有与穿戴装置相同的关节构型和外部尺寸。操作员穿戴装置采集四类体表接触操作任务的演示数据，随后训练模仿学习策略并部署至同构机器人，使其能够依据体表触觉观测执行操作。

Result: 在四类涉及体表接触的操作任务上成功完成演示采集、策略学习与机器人自主执行的全流程验证，证明 TWINS 能够统一支持体表接触操作的示教、学习和执行。

Conclusion: TWINS 为涉及体表接触的机器人操作提供了一套完整的同构遥操作与模仿学习框架，有效填补了现有系统在这一领域的空白，展示了触觉感知在全身接触操作中的关键作用。

Abstract: Recent advances in robot learning for manipulation have increased the importance of collecting real-world demonstration data. However, existing robotic systems primarily focus on end-effector manipulation, making it difficult to teach and execute manipulation tasks involving body-surface contact with the arms and chest. This paper presents TWINS (Tactile Wearable Isomorphic Arm Networked System), a robotic system for manipulation involving body-surface contact. TWINS consists of a Wearable Dual-Arm Device, which is worn by the operator, and an Isomorphic Robot with the same joint configuration and external dimensions. Distributed tactile sensors embedded in the chest and arms enable the measurement of body-surface contact synchronized with joint motion. Using the Wearable Dual-Arm Device, we collected demonstrations for four manipulation tasks involving body-surface contact. We then trained imitation learning policies using the collected demonstrations and deployed them on the Isomorphic Robot, enabling manipulation guided by body-surface tactile observations. Experimental results demonstrate that TWINS provides a unified robotic system for demonstration, learning, and execution of manipulation involving body-surface contact. https://mmurooka.github.io/twins-project-page/

</details>


### [74] [A Tilt-Rotor UAV with a Gripper for Stable Contact-Based Tasks via Environmental Anchoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01736)
*Joshua Taylor,Nursultan Imanberdiyev,Wei-Yun Yau,Guillaume Sartoretti,Efe Camci*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种配备倾转旋翼和欠驱动缆索驱动夹爪的新型无人机平台，可通过环境锚定从自由飞行过渡到稳定作业状态，实验表明锚定后位置漂移RMSE降低超95%，并能承受高达75N的纵向反作用力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中机器人在物理交互过程中保持稳定姿态是一个重大挑战，这限制了其在接触式任务中的应用。现有系统难以在受到外部反作用力时维持精确稳定的位姿，因此需要一种能够将飞行平台稳固锚定于环境的解决方案。

Method: 系统由两大核心组件构成：1）倾转旋翼机构的多旋翼飞行器，可将俯仰与前向运动解耦，实现在非零俯仰角下的稳定悬停；2）新型欠驱动、缆索驱动的棱柱形夹爪，具备顺应性以适应不规则几何表面，通过将无人机锚定于环境（如树枝、管道等）来稳定整个平台。论文完成了完整系统的设计与原型制作，并通过真实飞行测试验证性能。

Result: 实验结果表明：锚定后位置漂移RMSE相比自由飞行基线降低超过95%，即使在有风条件下依然有效；锚定系统可承受高达75N的纵向反作用力并保持稳定姿态；在多种目标几何形状和取向下，位置漂移RMSE始终不超过3mm。

Conclusion: 该方法在复杂物理交互任务中具有可行性，可应用于树木健康采样钻孔或难以到达位置的传感器安装等场景。

Abstract: Maintaining a stable pose during physical interaction is a significant challenge for aerial robots, often limiting their use in contact-based tasks. This paper presents a novel uncrewed aerial vehicle (UAV) platform designed to transition from unconstrained flight to a stable, constrained work platform via environmental anchoring. Our system comprises: 1) a multirotor with a tilt-rotor mechanism that decouples pitch from forward motion, enabling stable hover at non-zero pitch angles, and 2) a novel underactuated, cable-driven, prismatic gripper featuring compliance to adapt to irregular geometries, designed to stabilize the UAV by anchoring it to its environment. We present the design and prototyping of the complete system and validate its performance through a series of real-robot flight tests. Results demonstrate that anchoring significantly improves stability for interaction tasks, reducing positional drift RMSE by over 95% compared to a free-flight baseline, even under windy conditions. The anchored system can withstand longitudinal reaction forces up to 75N while maintaining a stable pose. Furthermore, across a range of target geometries and orientations, the system demonstrated consistent stability with a positional drift RMSE that never exceeded 3mm. These results establish the viability of our approach for complex physical interaction tasks, such as sampling tree health by drilling or sensor installation in hard-to-reach locations. Watch our UAV at: https://youtu.be/HDQ8S4ZW3Ls

</details>


### [75] [Hybrid Impedance-Admittance Control with Multi-Link Aerial Robot for Contact-Rich Surface Sliding Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01800)
*Zicheng Luo,Maolin Lei,Jinjie Li,Yicheng Chen,Zicen Xiong,Moju Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对多连杆空中机器人的混合阻抗-导纳控制策略，利用其关节式结构实现力与运动的解耦调控：通过关节角度调节产生导纳行为以实现顺应性适应，通过旋翼推力调节产生阻抗行为以维持扰动鲁棒性，从而在未知表面上实现稳健且自适应的滑动操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统空中机器人在执行接触丰富的操作（如表面滑动）时面临两难：阻抗控制可提供抗扰动能力但缺乏顺应性，导纳控制可提供顺应性但抗扰动能力弱。由于两者力-运动因果关系相反，在单一执行源（如旋翼推力）上无法同时实现。多连杆空中机器人具备关节和旋翼双执行源，为解耦实现两种控制范式提供了可能。

Method: 提出一种混合阻抗-导纳控制策略，利用多连杆空中机器人的关节式形态实现功能分离：通过关节角度调控生成导纳行为，实现对未知表面几何的顺应性交互；通过调节旋翼推力实现阻抗行为，调控滑动运动；二者协同工作，使机器人同时获得导纳的顺应性与阻抗的鲁棒性。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的混合控制策略使多连杆空中机器人能够在未知表面上实现鲁棒且顺应性的滑动性能。

Conclusion: 多连杆空中机器人的关节式结构为力与运动控制的解耦提供了硬件基础，混合阻抗-导纳控制框架有效融合了两种控制范式的互补优势，为接触丰富的空中操作任务提供了一种有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-link aerial robots can actively deform their articulated structures during flight, giving them strong potential for aerial manipulation. However, they still face substantial challenges in contact-rich aerial manipulation tasks such as surface sliding, which requires both disturbance robustness and compliance to uncertain surface geometry. Force-control strategies such as impedance and admittance control are commonly employed to address these requirements. Although impedance control can provide disturbance-resistant interaction and admittance control can offer compliant adaptation, their opposite force--motion causalities prevent their simultaneous implementation when applied through the same actuation source, such as the rotor thrusts used by conventional aerial robots. To overcome this limitation, we propose a hybrid impedance--admittance control strategy for a multi-link aerial robot. The articulated morphology enables a functional separation of force and motion regulation across joint and rotor actuation sources. In this framework, admittance behavior is generated through joint angle regulation to enhance adaptive interaction, while impedance behavior is achieved by modulating rotor thrust to regulate the sliding motion. This structural coordination allows the robot to leverage the complementary strengths of both control paradigms. As a result, the multi-link aerial robot achieves resilient and adaptive surface sliding. Experimental results demonstrate robust and compliant sliding performance on unknown surfaces.

</details>


### [76] [ReTouch: Empowering Contact-Rich Dexterous Manipulation with Online-Refined Tactile Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01824)
*Shiqi Zhang,Xin Zhang,Yedong Shen,Jiajun Deng,Yuxuan Gao,Sha Zhang,Yuan Zhang,Kaixue Long,Jiajia Wu,Jia Pan,Yao Li,Yanyong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReTouch 是一种支持接触丰富灵巧操作的视觉-语言-动作模型，通过在线触觉预测与闭环优化，在标准与挑战条件下分别超越最强基线 18.4 和 23.8 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉信号对接触丰富操作至关重要，但将触觉反馈有效融入灵巧操作仍未被充分探索。现有方法缺乏对触觉表征的结构化处理以及执行过程中的闭环反馈修正机制。

Method: 提出 ReTouch，包含两大创新：(1) Tactile-Patch Encoder，将触觉观测编码为保留手指身份和局部接触结构的触觉补丁特征；(2) 高频动作模块，联合预测未来触觉状态与动作块，并在执行过程中利用实时触觉反馈对两者进行持续优化，形成闭环修正。

Result: 在真实机器人平台上，ReTouch 在标准条件下平均成功率超越最强基线 18.4 个百分点，在挑战条件下超越 23.8 个百分点。同时构建了包含 900 个真实世界示教的 XHT-Dataset。

Conclusion: ReTouch 通过结构化触觉表征与闭环触觉预测优化，显著提升了接触丰富灵巧操作的鲁棒性与成功率，验证了在线触觉反馈对灵巧操控的关键价值。

Abstract: Fusing tactile signals has proven effective for contact-rich manipulation, enabling robots to perceive contact states and adapt to rapidly changing physical interactions. Yet effectively integrating tactile feedback into dexterous manipulation remains underexplored. In this work, we introduce ReTouch, a vision-language-action model (VLA) that supports contact-rich dexterous manipulation through tactile predictions continually refined online using execution-time feedback. ReTouch builds on two main innovations for tactile representation and closed-loop action generation. First, its Tactile-Patch Encoder represents tactile observations as structured tactile patch features that preserve finger identity and local contact structure, providing contact cues for fine-grained dexterous control. Second, its high-frequency action module jointly predicts future tactile states and action chunks and refines both using incoming tactile feedback during execution. This closed-loop refinement keeps tactile predictions aligned with evolving physical interactions, enabling responsive action correction and improving robustness to contact changes and execution errors. We further introduce XHT-Dataset, comprising 900 real-world demonstrations across seven contact-rich tasks collected on an XHand--UR7e platform, and evaluate ReTouch through closed-loop real-robot experiments. ReTouch surpasses the strongest baseline by 18.4 and 23.8 percentage points in average success rate under standard and challenging conditions, respectively, demonstrating its effectiveness and robustness.

</details>


### [77] [Multi-View Unified Camera Fields: Geometry-Shaped Action-Facing Representations for RGB-Only Multi-Camera VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01826)
*Jiarui Yang,Yehao Lu,Yuning Su,Yufeng Xie,Yu Zhong,Haiyu Lan,Tianjing Hao,Kaixiang Lu,Peiwen Lin,Chuang Wang,Enyu Li,Junwei Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MVUCF提出了一种训练阶段的多视角统一相机场框架，通过坐标查询深度目标和预处理感知对应目标，在不增加推理开销的情况下，为VLA模型注入几何一致性，显著提升了接触密集型操作任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多相机VLA模型通常仅简单拼接视角token，导致动作表征在度量深度上较弱且跨相机不一致，难以有效应对遮挡场景下的接触密集型操作任务。

Method: 提出MVUCF框架：1）构建跨视角共享的动作面向潜在场；2）引入坐标查询深度目标使度量深度可恢复；3）设计预处理感知对应目标对齐不同相机中观测同一物理点的token；4）训练后移除深度、相机标定和辅助头，部署时保持原始RGB图结构，无额外推理FLOPs。

Result: 在GR00T-N1.6匹配设置下，LIBERO达98.9%，LIBERO-Plus提升22.4个百分点；六个RoboTwin任务（涵盖触摸、移动-放置、接触交互三类动作族）成功率提升23.3个百分点；真实世界人形机器人实验进一步验证了RGB-only部署下的实际有效性。

Conclusion: MVUCF通过训练阶段注入多视角几何先验（深度一致性与跨视图对应），在不增加推理成本的前提下，显著增强了VLA模型在复杂接触密集型机器人操作中的泛化能力与任务成功率。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown strong generalization in robotic manipulation, yet complex contact-rich tasks often benefit from multi-camera observations that jointly capture the end effector, objects, and targets under occlusion. Existing multi-camera VLAs usually concatenate view tokens, leaving action representations weak in metric depth and inconsistent across cameras. We introduce Multi-View Unified Camera Fields (MVUCF), a training-only framework that forms a shared action-facing latent field across views. A coordinate-query depth objective makes metric depth recoverable, while a preprocessing-aware correspondence objective aligns tokens observing the same physical point from different cameras. Both directly shape the hidden states consumed by the action module. After geometry injection, depth, camera calibration, and auxiliary heads are removed, so deployment uses the original RGB-only graph with no extra inference FLOPs. Held-out probes confirm stronger depth recovery and cross-view matching. Under matched GR00T-N1.6 settings, MVUCF reaches 98.9% on LIBERO, improves LIBERO-Plus by 22.4 points, and raises success by 23.3 points across six RoboTwin tasks spanning three action families: touch, move-and-place, and contact interaction. Real-world humanoid experiments further provide evidence of its practical effectiveness under RGB-only deployment.

</details>


### [78] [Teleopit: A Full-Embodiment Humanoid Teleoperation System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01834)
*Bingqian Wu,Zicheng Xu,Xianghui Fan,Dayu Li,Xiangru Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Teleopit 是一个基于VR的全身具身遥操作系统，可同时映射人体、手部和头部信号到人形机器人的身体、可配置灵巧手和2-DOF主动视觉模块，并通过历史编码器和故障感知回退采样改进运动跟踪，支持无需调参的优化手部重定向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥操作演示采集系统要么简化手部指令，要么依赖专用可穿戴传感器获取精细手部运动，缺乏一种低成本、全身协调控制（包括身体运动、灵巧手操作和视角控制）的统一方案。

Method: 提出Teleopit系统，包含三大组件：（1）历史编码器与故障感知回退采样（failure-aware rewind sampling）改进的运动跟踪器，支持动捕和VR实时参考；（2）基于优化的手部重定向器，结合归一化手指方向、指尖闭合度和拇指坐标系对齐，将人手运动映射到不同灵巧手，无需针对特定手部调整目标函数或求解器超参数；（3）VR信号到人形机器人身体、灵巧手和2-DOF主动视觉模块的完整映射。

Result: 组件实验评估了跟踪成功率和重定向行为；真实机器人遥操作展示了协调的 locomotion、操作和视角控制。使用Teleopit采集的96次成功演示训练ACT和GR00T N1.7策略，在人形机器人上部署后分别达到90.0%和95.0%的任务成功率。

Conclusion: Teleopit 实现了低成本、全身协调的VR遥操作方案，采集的演示数据可有效训练模仿学习策略（ACT和GR00T N1.7），在人形机器人上获得高任务成功率，验证了系统作为演示采集平台的实用价值。

Abstract: Humanoid teleoperation for demonstration collection requires coordinated whole-body motion, continuous dexterous hand control, and viewpoint control. Existing systems either simplify hand commands or depend on dedicated wearable sensors for fine-grained hand motion. We introduce Teleopit, a full-embodiment teleoperation system that maps body, hand, and head signals from VR to a humanoid body, configurable dexterous hands, and a 2-DoF active vision module. A history encoder and failure-aware rewind sampling improve the motion tracker on both motion-capture and live VR references. An optimization-based hand retargeter combines normalized finger directions, fingertip closure, and thumb-frame alignment to map human hand motion to different dexterous hands without tuning hand-specific objective or solver hyperparameters. Component experiments evaluate tracking success rate and retargeting behavior, while real-robot teleoperation demonstrates coordinated locomotion, manipulation, and viewpoint control. ACT and GR00T N1.7 policies trained on 96 successful demonstrations collected with Teleopit achieve task success rates of 90.0% and 95.0%, respectively, when deployed on the humanoid. The project page is available at https://botrunner64.github.io/teleopit-page.

</details>


### [79] [Weights or Skills? A Survey of Robot-Learning Techniques: from Action-Predicting Weights to Robots that Write their Own Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01851)
*Gaytri Jena,Kapil Wanaskar,Vinija Jain,Aman Chadha,Vasu Sharma,Amitava Das*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文围绕“权重固化”与“可执行代码技能”两条技术路线，系统梳理了机器人学习领域。核心贡献在于提出一个以自我改进程度为轴心的分类法，将代码即策略方法从零样本合成到开放式进化循环进行排序，并厘清了“技能”一词的五种不同含义，最后将该分类法与新兴的机器人技能经济联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习正分化为两条路径：一是将能力固化在冻结权重中的VLA模型，二是将策略表达为可自我改进的代码。当前缺乏一个统一的框架来理解这两者之间的关系，特别是代码即策略方法中自我改进机制的光谱分布，以及“技能”一词在不同语境下的歧义问题。

Method: 本文采用聚焦式综述方法，对77个代表性系统进行分析。核心分析框架是将代码即策略方法按其自我改进程度排列：从零样本程序合成、闭环自我修复、持久技能记忆，到执行反馈、技能记忆与进化搜索三者结合的开放式循环。同时，将“技能”一词拆解为五种不同含义（无监督RL技能发现、LLM技能库等），并指出仅“代码意义”上的技能无需梯度更新即可自我改进。最后将该分类法与商业机器人技能市场中的适配性、跨本体可移植性、溯源、安全验证等问题进行关联。

Result: 研究发现，执行反馈、技能记忆与进化搜索三者结合的开放式循环这一象限几乎空白，仅有ASPIRE、ENPIRE和RoboClaw等少数近期系统占据该位置。“技能”一词在文献中存在至少五种不同用法，其中仅代码即策略范式支持无需梯度更新的自我改进。商业技能市场目前仅提供静态回放式的“一键部署”技能，暴露出适应性、跨本体可移植性、溯源、安全验证、组合性和标准化等开放问题。

Conclusion: 本文为机器人学习领域提供了“权重 vs. 技能”的清晰轴线和基于自我改进程度的分类法，填补了代码即策略方法光谱分析上的空白，并明确了技能经济中亟待解决的开放挑战。该分类法可作为理解和推进该领域研究的参考框架。

Abstract: Robot learning is splitting into two bets: policies that bake competence into frozen weights (vision-language-action, or VLA, models), and agents that write and refine their own executable skills as code. This survey organises the field around that axis of weights versus skills. Its central analytical contribution is a deep-dive that arranges code-as-policy methods by their degree of self-improvement, from zero-shot program synthesis, through closed-loop self-repair and persistent skill memory, to the sparsely populated cell in which execution feedback, skill memory, and evolutionary search combine into one open-ended loop; only a few very recent systems (for example ASPIRE, ENPIRE, and RoboClaw) occupy that cell. We map the complementary "skills" pole, from unsupervised reinforcement-learning skill discovery to large-language-model skill libraries, and show that the word "skill" is used in at least five distinct senses, of which only the code sense self-improves without gradient updates. We then connect the taxonomy to the emerging skill economy: commercial robot-skill marketplaces now distribute one-tap skills across robots but ship only static playback, which surfaces open problems of adaptation, cross-embodiment portability, provenance, safety verification, composition, and standardisation. This is a deliberately focused survey. Rather than cataloguing the field exhaustively, it examines 77 representative systems across six technique families through one taxonomy and a set of contrast tables, and it supplies operational definitions of the self-improvement mechanisms together with a statement of what each family cannot do.

</details>


### [80] [RADAR Perception for Dynamic Obstacle Avoidance onboard small-scale Quadrotor UAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01855)
*Dnyandeep Mandaokar,Bernhard Rinner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出首个基于毫米波雷达的机载感知与控制系统，用于无人机快速动态避障。系统采用交互多模型跟踪器和控制屏障函数控制器，在多种环境条件下实现亚米级定位精度，端到端感知到指令延迟仅约14毫秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速动态避障对无人机要求极高，既需要低延迟控制与驱动，也需要具备足够感知范围、能精确检测障碍物并估计其速度的可靠感知系统。现有方案在感知距离、速度估计和延迟方面存在不足。

Method: 采用毫米波雷达作为感知传感器，推导感知范围、相对速度与控制延迟之间的延迟与空间边界条件，给出成功避障的充分条件；设计基于交互多模型（IMM）的轻量级跟踪器和基于控制屏障函数（CBF）的控制器，直接输出避障加速度指令；系统部署在Raspberry Pi 4B上实现机载实时运行。

Result: 在三种不同尺寸物体、不同光照条件（明/暗）下进行300次实验，x、y、z方向的定位误差分别小于0.15 m、0.93 m和0.87 m；在烟雾环境中进行90次实验也获得相似误差分布。端到端感知到指令延迟约14 ms，验证了实时性。

Conclusion: 所提出的毫米波雷达感知与控制系统能够满足快速动态避障的低延迟和高精度要求，且不受光照和烟雾等视觉退化环境影响，代码与数据集已开源。

Abstract: Fast dynamic obstacle avoidance (DOA) on uncrewed aerial vehicles (UAVs) demands not only low-latency control and actuation but also reliable perception with sufficient sensing range for accurate obstacle detection and speed estimation. This letter presents, to the best of our knowledge, the first mmWave RADAR-based perception-and-control system for fast onboard DOA. We derive and analyze latency and spatial bounds that relate sensing range, relative speed, and control delay, yielding sufficient conditions for successful avoidance. Our system adopts a lightweight tracker based on interacting multiple models and a controller based on control-barrier functions that directly outputs evasive accelerations. It achieves position errors of less than 0.15 m, 0.93 m, and 0.87 m in x, y, and z directions for 300 experiments with three different object sizes and varying visibility (light and dark), and a similar spread for 90 experiments in smoke. An onboard implementation on a Raspberry Pi 4B demonstrates real-time feasibility with an end-to-end sensing-to-command latency of approximately 14 ms. Code and the full dataset of 390 throws are available (https://tinyurl.com/radardoagit).

</details>


### [81] [World Action Models in Real Time: An Empirical Study of Smooth Execution via Asynchronous Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01880)
*Motubrain Team*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文实证研究了六种异步部署策略，用于将高延迟的世界动作模型部署到实时机器人系统中，结果表明前缀条件生成在任务性能、执行速度和轨迹平滑度之间实现了最佳平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型通过迭代去噪生成固定时域的动作块，导致推理延迟高，在机器人执行过程中引发停顿、动作陈旧和轨迹不连续等问题。需要研究异步部署策略，使模型推理与动作执行重叠，以实现响应灵敏且平滑的控制。

Method: 在10 Hz双臂机器人平台上比较六种策略：同步执行、纯异步切换、事后动作混合、去噪时域混合、推理时速度引导和前缀条件生成。评估结合离线轨迹分析与在线实验，涵盖动态操作、精密放置和长时域任务。

Result: 观察、预测与执行命令之间的精确时间对齐是基本要求，对齐误差导致无法仅通过混合修正的动作块边界不连续。直接动作加权在正确对齐下简单平滑，但在精密任务中牺牲精度；推理时速度引导未能可靠约束已提交动作；前缀条件生成通过训练中学习一致的动作延续，实现最佳综合性能。

Conclusion: 前缀条件生成策略是实现高延迟世界动作模型实时部署的最优异步方案，研究结果为实际系统中的策略选择提供了明确指导。

Abstract: World Action Models generate fixed-horizon action chunks through iterative denoising, creating substantial inference latency that can cause pauses, stale actions, and discontinuities during robotic execution. We present an empirical study of asynchronous deployment strategies that overlap model inference with action execution to enable responsive and smooth control. We compare six strategies, including synchronous execution, pure asynchronous switching, post-hoc action blending, denoising-time blending, inference-time velocity guidance, and prefix-conditioned generation, on a 10 Hz bimanual robot. Evaluation combines offline trajectory analysis with online experiments across dynamic manipulation, precision-critical placement, and long-horizon tasks. Our results identify accurate temporal alignment between observations, predictions, and executed commands as a fundamental requirement. Alignment errors produce persistent chunk-boundary discontinuities that cannot be corrected through blending alone. With proper alignment, direct action weighting provides a simple and smooth baseline but sacrifices accuracy in precision-critical tasks. Inference-time velocity guidance fails to reliably constrain committed actions on our platform. In contrast, prefix-conditioned generation achieves the best overall balance between task performance, execution speed, and trajectory smoothness by learning consistent action continuations during training. These findings clarify the practical trade-offs among asynchronous deployment strategies and provide guidance for deploying high-latency World Action Models in real-time robotic systems.

</details>


### [82] [FRA-NBV: A Fast and Reflectivity-Aware Next-Best-View Strategy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01950)
*G. F. Preziosa,E. Setti,M. Faroni,A. M. Zanchettin,P. Rocco*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FRA-NBV是一种面向反射表面的快速反射感知最佳下一视角策略，通过在线椭球体建模和入射角调整，无需先验模型即可显著提升工业场景下深度传感器的三维重建覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反射表面导致深度传感器数据缺失或不可靠，严重削弱传统NBV策略的效果。工业应用中低成本低分辨率深度传感器对反射失效的鲁棒性需求迫切，但现有方法多依赖先验模型或材质假设，难以泛化。

Method: 提出FRA-NBV策略，包含三个核心步骤：(1) 利用缺失深度测量的空间分布识别反射区域；(2) 通过在线椭球体表示法将反射区域在三维空间中定位；(3) 执行恢复策略，选择额外采集姿态以改变传感器入射角，从而提高反射区域的重建概率。整个过程无需目标物体先验模型或材质反射率假设。

Result: 在具有不同几何复杂度和反射复杂度的物体上开展实验，结果表明该方法在真实工业条件下显著提升了重建覆盖率。

Conclusion: FRA-NBV无需先验模型即可有效应对反射引起的深度丢失问题，适用于广泛的工业配置，为低成本深度传感器的鲁棒三维重建提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Autonomous 3D reconstruction with depth sensors is strongly affected by reflective surfaces, which cause missing or unreliable measurements and reduce the effectiveness of conventional Next-Best-View (NBV) strategies. This limitation is particularly critical in industrial applications involving reflective components and low-cost, low-resolution depth sensing, where robustness to sensing failures is essential. This paper proposes a Fast Reflectivity-Aware Next-Best-View (FRA-NBV) strategy that explicitly addresses reflection-induced depth loss without relying on prior object models or assumptions on material reflectance, making it suitable for a wide range of industrial configurations. Reflective regions are identified from the spatial distribution of missing depth measurements and localized in three-dimensional space using an online ellipsoid-based representation of the object estimate. A recovery strategy then selects additional poses that modify the sensor's angle of incidence to improve the likelihood of reconstructing the affected regions. Experiments on objects with different geometric and reflective complexity demonstrate that the approach significantly improves reconstruction coverage under realistic industrial conditions.

</details>


### [83] [Roomer: Reflective Object-Grounded Model Editing and Repair for 3D Indoor Layout Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01973)
*Lingwei Dang,Ziyan Qiu,Jiajia Cheng,Shishuo Shang,Zhenhao Zhang,Yufei Zhu,Qingxin Xiao,Pan Liu,Shenghui Huang,Yun Hao,Juntong Li,Qingyao Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Roomer是一个反思式修复框架，将室内布局中的局部违规（碰撞、越界、遮挡开口、阻塞通道）建模为稀疏的、以物体为锚点的修复问题。通过RoState编码、RoReview绑定违规、几何条件视觉语言模型规划局部编辑、确定性求解器验证并生成候选方案，最终仅在全场景验证通过后提交修改。引入Roomer-CC训练数据集和Roomer-Eval评估基准，实验证明其能修复残余违规、保持有效区域、提升物理有效性和可用性，并可迁移至外部生成器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有室内布局生成器虽能产生全局合理的布局，但仍存在局部违规（碰撞、越界、开口遮挡、通道阻塞），且大多数工作缺乏对违规对象的定位和局部修复能力。

Method: 提出Roomer反思式修复框架：(1)RoState编码布局状态；(2)RoReview将违规测量绑定到涉事物体；(3)几何条件视觉语言模型规划器提出结构化局部编辑；(4)确定性求解器验证并生成候选编辑集合；(5)仅在全场景验证通过且无新硬违规或破坏受保护约束时提交编辑。训练数据来自Roomer-CC（受控破坏数据集），评估使用新基准Roomer-Eval。

Result: Roomer能修复残余违规并保留有效区域，提升物理有效性和可用性，且可迁移至外部生成器。Roomer-Eval从分布质量、物理有效性和实际可用性三个维度进行评估。

Conclusion: Roomer作为一种反思式局部修复框架，有效弥补了全局布局生成器在局部违规处理上的不足，通过结构化编辑和严格验证机制实现了可靠的布局修复，具有良好的泛化迁移能力。

Abstract: Existing indoor layout generators produce globally plausible layouts yet may retain local violations such as collisions, out-of-bounds placements, obstructed openings, and blocked circulation. Most prior work focuses on full-scene synthesis or scene-level optimization, with limited support for identifying responsible objects and locally repairing affected regions. We present Roomer, a reflective repair framework that casts these violations as sparse, object-grounded repair problems. Roomer encodes layouts as ``RoState'' and uses ``RoReview'' to bind measured violations to implicated objects. A geometry-conditioned vision-language model planner proposes a structured local edit, while a deterministic solver validates it and generates a finite set of candidate edits when needed. Each candidate is committed only if full-scene verification confirms that it resolves the target violation without new hard violations or broken protected constraints. We train the planner on Roomer-CC, a controlled-corruption dataset that pairs faulty layouts with object-grounded violation evidence and known-feasible inverse StatePatches. Since existing benchmarks rarely assess whether physically valid layouts are usable, we introduce Roomer-Eval to assess distributional quality, physical validity, and practical usability. Experiments show that Roomer repairs residual violations while preserving valid regions, improves physical validity and usability, and transfers across external generators.

</details>


### [84] [Adaptive Human-Robot Collaborative Painting Combining Preference-Based Optimization and Dynamic Motion Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01981)
*C. Cella,M. Ristic,M. Faroni,A. M. Zanchettin,P. Rocco*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种融合偏好优化（PBO）与动态运动基元（DMP）的人机协作框架，用于优化喷涂等机器人辅助任务。系统通过GLISp算法迭代优化控制参数，并改进DMP以增强机器人反应能力，实验表明该方法可有效降低操作员负担并优化任务效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人辅助作业（如喷涂）中，如何使机器人实时适应操作员的手部姿态、减少操作员体力负担，同时保证作业质量，是一个关键挑战。现有方法在兼顾操作员偏好与机器人实时响应方面存在不足，因此需要一种以人为本的协作优化框架。

Method: 提出的人机协作框架包含两大核心模块：（1）基于偏好的优化（PBO）：采用GLISp（基于偏好学习的全局优化）算法，通过人类反馈迭代优化执行时间、机器人响应速度、旋转放大系数等控制参数；（2）改进型动态运动基元（DMP）：对传统DMP进行修改，增强机器人对操作员动作的反应能力及对工效学需求的适应性。系统允许操作员自由执行任务，机器人实时调整工件朝向以匹配操作员手部方向。

Result: 在由不同背景参与者组成的异质群体中开展了喷涂任务验证实验。结果表明，所提策略能够有效降低操作员的体力负担（effort），同时优化喷涂作业的工艺效果。

Conclusion: 该框架成功将人类偏好纳入机器人控制回路，实现了以人为本的自适应协作。PBO与改进DMP的结合使系统在实时性、工效学适应性和任务质量方面均取得良好表现，验证了该方法在机器人辅助作业场景中的有效性。

Abstract: This work presents a human-centered collaborative framework that integrates Preference-Based Optimization (PBO) and Dynamic Movement Primitives (DMPs) to optimize robot-assisted tasks such as painting. The system allows the operator to perform the process while the robot adapts its behavior in real-time, dynamically adjusting the orientation of the piece in order to match the orientation of the operator's hand. The PBO framework leverages the GLISp algorithm to iteratively refine control parameters such as execution time, robot responsiveness, and rotation amplification through human feedback. Moreover, DMPs have been modified to enhance the reactive behavior of the robot and its adaptability to ergonomic requirements. The method was validated with a heterogeneous group of participants executing \rev{painting tasks}. The results show that our strategy effectively reduces operator effort while optimizing process outcomes.

</details>


### [85] [MANGO-Grasp: Mahalanobis Fields over Geometry-Oriented 3D Gaussians for Cross-Embodiment Dexterous Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02014)
*Heng Zhang,Kevin Yuchen Ma,Mike Zheng Shou,Weisi Lin,Yan Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MANGO-Grasp 提出一种各向异性交互框架，将物体表示为几何导向的3D高斯原语、机械手表示为形态-运动学关键点描述符，通过马氏距离场建模接触方向性，实现跨具身灵巧抓取，在仿真和真实实验中均取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互中心方法存在两个不足：物体表征未能充分建模局部表面几何，机器人描述符未同时显式编码形态学与运动学信息。跨具身灵巧抓取需要一种能统一表征异构多指手、且捕捉接触方向性结构的框架。

Method: 提出MANGO-Grasp框架：(1) 物体端：根据几何复杂度自适应分配3D高斯原语，成形为沿表面法向对齐的板状结构，编码局部几何；(2) 机械手端：将手部表面关键点编码为形态-运动学描述符；(3) 交互建模：在关键点-原语对上构建马氏距离场作为训练目标，该场沿法向急剧上升、切向平缓，匹配接触的方向性结构；(4) 推理时以该场为优化引导，所有具身共享同一优化公式和超参数。

Result: 在CMAP和MultiGripperGrasp基准上，MANGO-Grasp较最强seen-hand基线提升8.24个百分点；在未见过的SharpaWave手上零样本迁移，较最强零样本基线提升16.57个百分点；真实世界实验成功率达86%。

Conclusion: MANGO-Grasp通过几何导向的各向异性交互建模，有效统一了跨具身灵巧抓取，在仿真和真实场景中均展现出优异的泛化能力和抓取成功率。

Abstract: Cross-embodiment dexterous grasping aims to synthesize stable grasps across heterogeneous multi-fingered hands with little or no embodiment-specific tuning. Existing interaction-centric methods achieve promising results, but their object representations often underrepresent local surface geometry, while their robot descriptors do not explicitly encode both robot morphology and kinematics. We propose MANGO-Grasp, an anisotropic interaction framework that represents objects as geometry-oriented 3D Gaussian primitives and robot hands as surface keypoints encoded into morpho-kinematic descriptors. The object primitives are adaptively allocated by geometric complexity and shaped as surface-aligned plates with outward normals, encoding local geometry. Mahalanobis fields over keypoint--primitive pairs serve as interaction prediction targets during training and as optimization guidance for grasp realization at inference. These fields rise sharply for displacement along the surface normal but only gently within the tangent plane, matching the directional structure of contact. Grasps are realized with one shared optimization formulation and hyperparameter setting across all embodiments. On the CMAP and MultiGripperGrasp benchmarks, MANGO-Grasp outperforms the strongest seen-hand baseline by up to 8.24 percentage points in simulation. It also transfers zero-shot to the unseen SharpaWave hand, improving over the strongest zero-shot baseline by up to 16.57 percentage points, and achieves 86% success in real-world experiments. The code and additional materials will be made available upon publication at https://connor-zh.github.io/MANGO-Grasp/.

</details>


### [86] [Open-DiffLoco: Open-Source Differentiable Learning for Deployable Blind Quadruped Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02069)
*Martin Opat*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Open-DiffLoco 是一个开源框架，利用可微仿真（MJX）和 SHAC 算法训练可部署的盲四足运动策略，无需复杂奖励工程和参考轨迹，在 Go2 机器人上实现鲁棒部署，训练仅需 20-60 分钟、6GB 显存，并提出 JAVE 改进策略梯度训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习训练可部署运动策略需要复杂奖励工程和高昂训练时间；可微仿真虽然高效，但缺乏能端到端迁移到物理硬件的开源工具。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在 MuJoCo XLA (MJX) 中实现 Short-Horizon Actor-Critic (SHAC) 算法，训练本体感知（盲）策略，移除特权观测（如基座线速度），不依赖参考轨迹，使用极简奖励函数。提出 Jacobian-Augmented Value Estimation (JAVE)，通过监督 Critic 雅可比矩阵改善早期一阶策略梯度训练。

Result: 在 Unitree Go2 四足机器人上部署，全向速度跟踪 RMSE 低于 0.2 m/s，最高速度超 1 m/s，对不平坦地形和侧向推挤等外部扰动具有鲁棒性。训练在单张 RTX 5080 上使用不到 6GB 显存，约 20-60 分钟完成。

Conclusion: Open-DiffLoco 是首个基于可微仿真的开源可部署运动策略训练框架，验证了可微仿真在真实机器人运动控制中的可行性与高效性。

Abstract: Developing deployable locomotion policies through conventional reinforcement learning often requires complex reward engineering and expensive training times. While differentiable simulation offers a highly efficient alternative, open-source tools capable of end-to-end transfer of these policies to physical hardware remain limited. This paper introduces Open-DiffLoco, an open-source framework for training deployable blind quadruped locomotion policies with differentiable simulation. The framework implements the Short-Horizon Actor-Critic (SHAC) algorithm in MuJoCo XLA (MJX) and trains a proprioceptive policy that transfers to real-world hardware. The deployed policy removes privileged actor observations, including base linear velocity, and does not rely on reference trajectories. It also uses a substantially simplified reward function, enabling the robot to discover walking patterns without the complex auxiliary rewards typically used in conventional reinforcement learning pipelines. When deployed on physical hardware (a Unitree Go2 quadruped), the trained policy tracks omnidirectional velocity commands with root-mean-square error below 0.2 m/s, reaches speeds above 1 m/s, and remains robust to uneven terrain and external physical disturbances, such as lateral pushes. Across the reported configurations, training uses under 6 GB of VRAM on a single NVIDIA GeForce RTX 5080 GPU and completes in approximately 20-60 minutes. As an algorithmic extension to SHAC, we propose Jacobian-Augmented Value Estimation (JAVE), which supervises the critic Jacobians to improve early first-order policy-gradient training. To our knowledge, Open-DiffLoco is the first open-source framework for training deployable locomotion policies using differentiable simulation. Deployment videos and source code are available at: https://diffloco.martin-opat.com/

</details>


### [87] [TANGO-VIO: Triangulation-Aware Navigation with Guaranteed Feature-Observability for Visual-Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02079)
*Ege C. Altunkaya,Abdülbaki Şanlan,Emre Koyuncu,İbrahim Özkol*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TANGO-VIO 提出了一个三角化感知的导航框架，通过将特征堆叠方位矩阵的对数行列式度量嵌入控制屏障函数中，以主动维护视觉-惯性里程计中特征的可观测性，从而在低视差运动下改善三角化条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在视觉导航与VIO中，纯旋转或无足够视差时三角化会病态甚至不可行，但现有文献对导航过程中主动维护特征可观测性的研究不足，亟需一种能在运动控制层面保障三角化质量的框架。

Method: 提出TANGO-VIO框架：定义特征级堆叠方位矩阵的对数行列式作为三角化信息度量，将其嵌入控制屏障函数(CBF)中，通过名义方向加权的最小偏差速度修正，为聚合三角化信息度量施加下界约束，从而在特征几何意义上保障可观测性。

Result: 软件在环仿真和真实飞行实验表明，该方法在低视差运动下显著改善了三角化条件数，飞行响应与仿真行为高度一致，验证了所提安全滤波器的实际可实现性。

Conclusion: TANGO-VIO通过控制屏障函数主动维护三角化可观测性，有效缓解了低视差场景下的退化问题，为VIO导航提供了一种具备实用性的安全保障机制。

Abstract: In vision-aided navigation and visual-inertial odometry, the quality of triangulated three-dimensional feature positions is a fundamental prerequisite for state estimation accuracy. Triangulation becomes ill-conditioned or even impossible when a camera undergoes pure rotation without translation, or when the observed bearing vectors provide insufficient parallax. Even though visual-inertial odometry has been extensively studied, the active maintenance of feature-observability during navigation has not been sufficiently addressed in the literature. To address this gap, this study presents TANGO-VIO, a triangulation-aware navigation framework that embeds a log-determinant metric of the feature-wise stacked-bearing matrix into a control barrier function. In this proposed method, the observability guarantee is established in the feature-geometric sense by enforcing a lower bound on the aggregate triangulation-information metric through a nominal-direction-weighted minimum-deviation velocity correction. The proposed architecture is evaluated through software-inthe- loop simulations and real flight experiments. The results show improved triangulation conditioning under low-parallax motion, while the flight response closely reproduces the corresponding simulation behavior and confirms the practical realizability of the proposed safety filter. Supplementary materials are available on the project webpage.

</details>


### [88] [Toward Geometry-Scalable Whole-Body Touch for Humanoids: A 3D-Printed Conformal EIT Skin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02080)
*Haofeng Chen,Carson Kohlbrenner,Jiri Kubik,Lukas Rustler,Alexander Dickhans,Karel Bartunek,Alessandro Roncone,Hyosang Lee,Matej Hoffmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于增材制造（3D打印）的共形电阻抗断层成像（EIT）触觉皮肤，利用柔性导电TPU层和接触增强贴片，通过一步高斯-牛顿EIT求解器实现接触定位。在平面、U形曲面及iCub面部形状上验证，曲面传感器在18个接触位置的平均定位误差仅6 mm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统触觉传感阵列在覆盖大面积、处理复杂曲面和布线复杂度方面扩展性差，且需针对特定机器人形态进行定制化重新设计。因此需要一种能够适应任意几何形状、制造简便、可规模化的大面积触觉传感方案，以满足人形机器人在接触密集型环境中运行的前提需求。

Method: 采用增材制造工作流，将柔性导电TPU（热塑性聚氨酯）作为连续传感域，表面布置导电接触增强贴片。当外部接触使贴片与导电层耦合时，引起边界电压变化，通过一步高斯-牛顿EIT求解器重建接触位置。研究中首先表征了层状结构的机电设计空间，验证了低电阻接触增强贴片和多孔导电TPU传感层对灵敏度的提升，并在平面、U形曲面和类iCub面部三种原型上进行接触定位验证。

Result: 低电阻接触增强贴片和多孔导电TPU层可有效提升灵敏度并保持可打印性。曲面传感器在18个接触位置上实现平均定位误差6 mm，无需监督式后处理。iCub面部形状原型也定性验证了该方法的几何适应性。

Conclusion: 增材制造的断层成像触觉皮肤能够显著降低针对特定形态的人形机器人触觉覆盖重新设计负担，为面向人机交互的大面积接触感知提供了一条实用路径。

Abstract: Whole-body tactile sensing is a prerequisite for humanoids that operate in contact-rich human environments, but conventional taxel arrays scale poorly with surface area, wiring complexity, and robot-specific curvature. We present a conformal electrical impedance tomography tactile skin fabricated through a geometry-adaptable additive-manufacturing workflow. A flexible conductive TPU layer forms a continuous sensing domain, while contact-induced coupling with conductive patches produces boundary voltage changes that are reconstructed using a one-step Gauss-Newton EIT solver. We first characterize the electromechanical design space of the layered structure and show that low-resistance contact-enhancement patches and a porous conductive TPU sensing layer improve sensitivity while preserving printability. We then validate contact localization on a planar prototype, a curved U-shaped prototype, and a qualitative iCub-face-shaped geometry. The curved sensor achieves a mean localization error of 6 mm over 18 contact positions without supervised post-processing. These results suggest that additively manufactured tomographic skins can reduce the morphology-specific redesign burden for humanoid tactile coverage and provide a practical route toward large-area contact sensing for human-centered deployment.

</details>


### [89] [Dynamic UAV-based search operations using probabilistic diffusion modeling of Man Overboard incident victims](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02093)
*Dimosthenis Angelis,Evangelos Boukas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合扩展卡尔曼滤波与Leeway模型的落水人员漂移预测方法，并评估了五种无人机搜索策略，其中改进概率信息搜索(IPIS)方法在延时20分钟、检测率仅30%的条件下仍能达到80%以上的搜救成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2010-2019年间超过70%的邮轮落水者丧生，亟需一种能够可靠预测落水人员漂移区域并高效执行搜索的方法，以提高海上搜救的成功率。

Method: 采用扩展卡尔曼滤波(EKF)，结合Leeway漂移模型对落水人员在海洋中的漂移轨迹进行预测，同时考虑人员运动的不确定性和气象条件。在此基础上，部署无人机(UAV)对预测的动态搜索区域进行覆盖搜索，评估了五种搜索方法：Zigzag、Boustrophedon、Spiral、概率信息搜索(PIS)以及改进概率信息搜索(IPIS)。

Result: IPIS方法表现最优，即使在无人机于落水事件发生20分钟后才启动搜索、且检测方法成功率仅为30%的情况下，平均搜索成功率仍超过80%。

Conclusion: 结合EKF漂移预测与IPIS搜索策略的无人机搜救方案，能够在实际约束条件下实现高效可靠的落水人员定位，显著提升海上生存救援能力。所有仿真代码已开源。

Abstract: More than 70% of the people that fell overboard cruise ships in the period 2010-2019 lost their lives. This paper presents a strategy for reliably predicting the area a person may be in after a man overboard incident, and describes in detail the search methods to find them utilizing UAV technology. The search area prediction method employs an Extended Kalman Filter that capitalizes on the information from the Leeway model to track the missing person in the sea by taking into account the uncertainty of the movement of the person and the weather conditions in the area. Then, a UAV uses this information to search for the person. Five different methods for searching in this dynamic area are presented and evaluated - the Zigzag, the Boustrophedon, the Spiral, the Probability Informed Search and the Improved Probability Informed Search (IPIS) methods. The IPIS method provides success rate of over 80% on average for finding a person, even if the UAV initiates the search mission 20 minutes after the man overboard incident and even assuming a detection method with a success rate of 30%. All code for the simulation environment and the evaluation of the methods is available on our GitHub page at https://github.com/diangeli/pdms-man-overboard.

</details>


### [90] [Look Where It Matters: Adaptive Visual Refinement for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02197)
*Jin Cui,Yanbin Hu,Xinyue Long,Linkai Li,Boran Zhao,Pengju Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AtVLA 通过在视觉编码器中插入可学习的寄存器token，解决VLA模型中注意力伪影导致的空间定位不可靠问题，并结合不确定性门控的局部高分辨率细化，显著提升了机器人操作精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型的视觉表征在空间精确操作中不可靠。作者发现VLA中的视觉编码器存在与通用ViT类似的注意力伪影，且这些伪影与后训练阶段获取的空间感知能力（物体定位、深度排序、局部几何）密切相关——有限的全局token容量迫使任务相关信息溢出到低信息量的patch token中，损害了精确目标定位和细粒度接触所需的干净注意力分布。

Method: 提出AtVLA框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 注意力修正：在视觉编码器中插入可学习的寄存器token，仅用具身数据和原始动作目标端到端训练，使寄存器成为具身空间信息的专用载体，恢复patch token干净、空间忠实的注意力分布；(2) 不确定性门控的局部细化：动作专家采样多个动作块，通过分歧估计不确定性；仅对不确定预测，利用动作条件注意力展开识别任务相关区域，裁剪后高分辨率重编码，追加到缓存前缀中生成细化动作。

Result: 在LIBERO基准上平均成功率从94.2%提升至98.4%；在真实世界单视角基准上从46.5%提升至69.0%。裁剪仅在约30%的重规划步骤触发，总计算量仅为基座模型的1.4-1.6倍。

Conclusion: 注意力修正与按需高分辨率细化的结合是一种高效且有效的策略，显著提升了VLA模型的空间操作精度，同时保持可接受的计算开销。

Abstract: Visual representations of VLA models remain unreliable for spatially precise robotic manipulation. We uncover that vision encoders in VLAs also exhibit attention artifacts previously documented in generic Vision Transformers, and further show that, in embodied policies, these artifacts are closely associated with spatial perception capabilities acquired during post-training. As the encoder learns task-relevant information such as object location, depth ordering, and local geometry, limited global-token capacity causes part of this information to spill into low-information patch tokens. We introduce AtVLA, a framework that inserts learnable register tokens into the visual encoder. Trained end-to-end using only embodied data and the original action objective, these registers emerge as dedicated carriers of embodied spatial information, while the remaining patch tokens recover clean and spatially faithful attention distributions crucial for precise target localization and fine-grained contact. Clean attention restores reliable localization, but cannot recover geometric details lost in low-resolution observations. AtVLA therefore couples attention rectification with uncertainty-gated local refinement. The action expert samples multiple action chunks and estimates uncertainty from their disagreement; only for uncertain predictions, action-conditioned attention rollout identifies the task-relevant region, which is cropped, re-encoded at high resolution, and appended to the cached prefix for refined action generation. Across LIBERO, SimplerEnv, and a challenging single-view real-world benchmark, AtVLA improves the average LIBERO success rate from 94.2% to 98.4% and real-world success from 46.5% to 69.0%. The cropping is triggered on approximately 30% of replanning steps, resulting in only 1.4-1.6x the total computation of the base model under the representative deployment setting.

</details>


### [91] [Learning Panorama-Aware VLA for Mobile Manipulation with Whole-Body Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02257)
*Donglin Yang,Haoran Chen,Xingyu Chen,Lixing Liu,Manyi Li,Changhe Tu,Ke Xu,Xiaojian Ma,Si Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对移动双臂操作任务，本文提出了全身遥操作系统与全景感知的VLA策略PanoVLA。该系统通过单一VR接口协调轮式双臂机器人，采集了5.5小时多模态演示数据集；PanoVLA基于Mixture-of-Transformers架构，引入全景编码与融合模块，在四项真实任务中取得91.3%平均阶段完成率和73.4%端到端成功率，显著优于局部视角基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动操作面临两大挑战：(1) 数据层面，高质量全身演示需要协调移动底盘与机械臂的联合控制，采集效率低；(2) 模型层面，现有VLA策略主要依赖局部相机观测，视野受限，阻碍全局空间理解能力。

Method: 首先开发了全身遥操作系统，通过单一VR接口实现对轮式双臂机器人的协调控制，并采集了5.5小时真实世界多模态演示数据集。基于该数据集，提出PanoVLA——一种全景感知的VLA策略，采用Mixture-of-Transformers架构，引入专用的全景编码和融合模块，将全局空间上下文与语言指令、机器人状态有效整合，用于动作生成。

Result: 在四项真实世界移动操作任务上，PanoVLA平均阶段完成率达91.3%，端到端成功率达73.4%，显著优于仅使用局部视角的基线方法。

Conclusion: 引入全景空间上下文能够显著提升移动机器人的空间理解能力与闭环操作性能，验证了全景感知在移动双臂操作中的有效性。

Abstract: Mobile manipulation is a key capability for embodied intelligence, enabling robots to accomplish complex multi-stage tasks in open-world environments. However, mobile manipulation poses two key challenges for vision-language-action (VLA) policies: At the data level, the efficient collection of high-quality whole-body demonstrations demands the coordinated control of both the mobile base and the robotic arms; at the model level, existing VLA models predominantly rely on local camera observations, whose limited field of view hinders global spatial understanding. To address these challenges, we develop a whole-body teleoperation system and a panoramic-aware VLA policy. The system enables coordinated control of a wheeled bimanual robot through a single VR interface and supports the acquisition of a real-world mobile manipulation dataset comprising 5.5 hours of multimodal demonstrations. Building upon this dataset, we propose PanoVLA, a panorama-aware vision-language-action policy for mobile bimanual manipulation. Built upon a Mixture-of-Transformers architecture, PanoVLA introduces global spatial context through dedicated panorama encoding and fusion modules, enabling effective integration of panoramic observations with language instructions and robot states for action generation. Evaluation on four real-world mobile manipulation tasks demonstrates that PanoVLA achieves an average stage completion rate of 91.3\% and an end-to-end success rate of 73.4\%, substantially outperforming local-view baselines. These results demonstrate that incorporating panoramic spatial context improves spatial understanding and closed-loop manipulation performance in mobile robots.

</details>


### [92] [TS-MAMP: A Remanufactured Agricultural Robot Powered by Second-Life EV Components and NMS-Free On-Device Weed Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02270)
*Weijie Shi,Zicheng Xu,Zhenbang Cheng,Haoran Xuan,Mingbo Duan,Gan Ge*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TS-MAMP是一种基于循环经济3R原则的再制造农业机器人平台，利用退役低速电动车（LSEV）动力总成部件（轮毂电机、铅酸电池）大幅降低BOM成本至450美元以下，并搭载YOLOv10n实现作物-杂草检测，为小农户提供可负担的AI农业自动化方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前Agriculture 4.0机器人系统对小农户而言资本密集度过高、难以负担；同时大量退役低速电动车动力总成仍具机电价值却被破坏性回收。本文旨在弥合这一鸿沟，通过再制造途径为碎片化的小农地块提供低成本农业机器人。

Method: 1）利用反电动势匹配配对退役48V无刷直流轮毂电机；2）筛选健康状态60%-80%的铅酸电池模组并进行主动均衡（模间电压偏差≤100mV）；3）桁架底盘设计支持≥200kg静载荷、1200-2000mm连续可调轮距、≤5分钟模块更换；4）采用NMS-free YOLOv10n检测器，结合一致双分配训练和负样本学习，在Jetson Nano上以FP16 TensorRT部署。

Result: 再制造使动力总成与底盘BOM成本降低约60%，降至450美元以下（不含感知和除草模块）；YOLOv10n在Wanxi作物-杂草数据集上达到80.87% mAP@0.5（58.41% mAP@0.5:0.95），并在Jetson Nano上验证了端侧推理可行性。

Conclusion: 退役电动车部件经适度筛选后可重新工程化为可负担的AI农业机器人，为被商业自动化忽视的小农地块开辟了一条可行的再制造路径。

Abstract: Agriculture 4.0 robotic systems improve field efficiency yet remain too capital-intensive for the fragmented smallholdings that dominate global agriculture. Meanwhile, a growing number of retired low-speed electric-vehicle (LSEV) powertrains retain functional electromechanical value but are destructively recycled. This paper presents TS-MAMP (Telescopic-Sleeve Modular Agricultural Mobile Platform), a remanufactured robot built under 3R (reduce, reuse, recycle) circular-economy principles. Retired 48 V brushless-DC (BLDC) hub motors are paired via back-EMF matching, and lead-acid battery modules screened at 60%-80% state of health are actively balanced within a 100 mV inter-module voltage deviation. Together, these reused components reduce the powertrain-and-chassis BOM cost by approximately 60%, to below USD 450 (perception and weeding modules excluded). The truss chassis provides >=200 kg static load, continuously adjustable track width from 1200 mm to 2000 mm, and <=5-minute module changeover. An NMS-free (non-maximum-suppression-free) YOLOv10n detector with consistent dual-assignment training and negative-sample learning achieves 80.87% mean average precision (mAP)@0.5 (58.41% mAP@0.5:0.95) on the Wanxi Crop-Weed dataset, and is deployed via FP16 TensorRT on a Jetson Nano, confirming on-device inference feasibility. TS-MAMP demonstrates that retired EV components, under modest screening, can be re-engineered into affordable, AI-enabled agricultural robots--opening a remanufacturing pathway for the smallholder fields that commercial automation leaves unserved.

</details>


### [93] [TRACE: Ergodic Trajectory Optimization for Active Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02304)
*Ziyue Zheng,Linli Shi,Bingkun He,Wen Jiang,Ziyun Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TRACE将主动重建建模为遍历覆盖问题，利用当前地图的不确定性和可见性在线推导目标信息分布，并通过核遍历视界规划器计算遍历轨迹，闭环连接建图与轨迹优化，在Replica数据集上相比NBV基线提升PSNR 1.5 dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于高斯泼溅的主动重建系统采用贪婪策略，每步仅优化单一NBV并通过短视距路径规划连接，这种贪婪解耦忽视了场景信息的全局结构，导致轨迹低效、在视图间转运时浪费传感能力。

Method: 将主动重建形式化为遍历覆盖问题：要求传感器轨迹的时间平均空间统计与当前地图导出的目标信息分布相匹配。在线从不确定性和可见性导出目标分布，采用基于核遍历视界规划器（结合梯度流与足迹耗尽）计算遍历轨迹，实现建图与轨迹优化的闭环。

Result: 在Replica数据集上与NBV基线进行充分对比评估，PSNR提升1.5 dB。

Conclusion: TRACE通过遍历覆盖框架克服了传统贪婪NBV方法的全局低效问题，实现了更优的主动重建质量。

Abstract: Existing active reconstruction systems with Gaussian-splatting maps select observations greedily, optimizing a single next-best-view (NBV) at each step and connecting the chosen views by short-horizon path planning. This greedy decoupling disregards the global structure of scene information, producing inefficient trajectories that waste sensing capacity in transit between selected views. In this work, we study active reconstruction as an ergodic coverage problem: the time-averaged spatial statistics of the sensor trajectory should match a target information distribution induced by the current map. Our approach derives this target distribution online from uncertainty and visibility, and calculates ergodic trajectories via a kernel-ergodic horizon planner with gradient flow and footprint depletion, closing the loop between mapping and trajectory optimization. We thoroughly evaluate TRACE on the Replica dataset against the Next-Best-View (NBV) baselines, improving PSNR by 1.5 dB. Code: https://github.com/spikelab-jhu/trace-active-reconstruction.

</details>


### [94] [Residual-Based Adaptive Kalman Filtering for Legged Robot State Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02316)
*Mihaela Popescu,Dennis Mronga,Shivesh Kumar,Frank Kirchner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对足式机器人状态估计的在线噪声协方差自适应方法，基于滤波残差和创新在线调整Q和R矩阵，在Invariant Extended Kalman Filter框架下实现。实验表明仅自适应R即可在trotting步态下提升25%精度，且无需足底力传感器即可达到与基于力传感器方法相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统卡尔曼滤波器中过程噪声协方差Q和测量噪声协方差R的调参依赖专家经验且需固定参数，难以适应足式机器人多变步态和环境的实际需求，因此需要一种在线自适应调整策略。

Method: 提出基于滤波残差（residual）和新息（innovation）的协方差自适应方法，在融合IMU与腿部运动学的Invariant Extended Kalman Filter（InEKF）框架内在线更新Q和R矩阵，并与依赖IMU和足底力测量的基线方法进行对比。

Result: 在Unitree Go2四足机器人的室内外数据集上，仅自适应R即可使trotting步态估计精度相比固定参数InEKF提升25%；基于残差的自适应方法在无需足底力传感器及额外调参的条件下，达到了与基于足底力方法相当的性能。

Conclusion: 所提在线噪声协方差自适应策略有效提升足式机器人状态估计精度和鲁棒性，且仅自适应R即可获得显著收益，避免了依赖力传感器带来的硬件成本和调参负担。

Abstract: State estimation is a key component in model-based control of walking robots and, more broadly, applicable wherever hidden variables must be inferred. The Kalman filter is widely used to estimate floating-base position and velocity by fusing multiple sensing modalities. However, tuning noise parameters is challenging and typically requires expert knowledge. Moreover, fixed noise parameters are unsuitable for varying gaits and environments. We propose an online adaptation strategy for the process noise covariance matrix Q and the measurement noise covariance matrix R. Specifically, we introduce a filter residual and innovation-based covariance adaptation method for legged robot state estimation and evaluate it against a baseline approach relying on IMU and foot force measurements. The proposed adaptation is implemented within an Invariant Extended Kalman Filter (InEKF) fusing IMU and leg kinematics. Experiments on indoor and outdoor datasets with a Unitree Go2 quadruped show that adapting R is sufficient and improves accuracy by 25% for the trotting gait compared to the fixed-tuned InEKF. Finally, the proposed residual-based adaptation achieves comparable performance to the foot force approach, without requiring foot force measurements or additional parameter tuning.

</details>


### [95] [TravKAN: Fast and Interpretable Nonlinear Traversability Analysis with Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02320)
*Daniel Fusaro,Simone Mosco,Wanmeng Li,Alberto Pretto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TravKAN 是一种基于 Kolmogorov-Arnold 网络的可通行性估计框架，通过可学习的一元函数组合实现紧凑架构，支持训练后符号化解析提取。同时首次系统性地利用 LiDAR 反射率通道构建手工特征，在公共数据集上性能接近 XGBoost，并具备高可解释性与实时推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的机器学习可通行性分析方法（如深度神经网络、梯度提升树）虽预测性能强，但缺乏可解释性，难以揭示地形-机器人交互的内在动力学。同时，LiDAR 反射率信息在手工业特征构建中长期未被系统性利用。

Method: 提出 TravKAN 框架，以 Kolmogorov-Arnold 网络替代传统 MLP，将多元决策函数分解为可学习的一元函数组合，实现紧凑架构与训练后符号公式提取（TravKAN-Lite）。同时首次系统性设计基于 LiDAR 反射率通道的手工特征集，作为几何特征的补充。在公开城市与越野数据集上进行评估，并与多种基线对比，辅以消融实验。

Result: TravKAN 在所有指标上表现强劲，优于传统深度模型，性能接近 XGBoost。其符号化版本 TravKAN-Lite 揭示了有意义的非线性特征交互，提供紧凑、可部署、快速的解析模型。消融实验验证了方法对架构变化的鲁棒性以及反射率特征的贡献。

Conclusion: TravKAN 兼顾高性能、可解释性与计算效率，非常适合安全关键场景下对透明度、实时性和可解释性有要求的机器人系统。

Abstract: Traversability analysis is a fundamental capability for autonomous mobile robots operating in unstructured environments. While modern machine learning approaches such as deep neural networks and gradient-boosted trees achieve strong predictive performance, they lack interpretability and provide limited insight into the underlying terrain-robot interaction dynamics. In this paper, we propose TravKAN, a Kolmogorov-Arnold Network-based framework for fast, scalable, and interpretable traversability estimation. TravKAN represents multivariate decision functions through compositions of learnable univariate functions, enabling compact architectures and symbolic extraction of analytic expressions after training. In addition, we introduce a novel set of handcrafted features derived from the reflectivity channel of LiDAR sensors. To the best of our knowledge, reflectivity has not been systematically exploited for handcrafted traversability descriptors, despite its potential to capture material and surface properties complementary to geometric cues. We evaluate TravKAN on public, real-world urban and off-road datasets and compare it against strong baselines. TravKAN achieves strong performance across all metrics, outperforming conventional deep models and approaching the performance of XGBoost. TravKAN-Lite, i.e., TravKAN's symbolic representation, reveals meaningful nonlinear feature interactions and provides a compact, deployment-friendly, and fast analytic model. Ablation studies further show the robustness of our method to architectural variations and quantify the contribution of the proposed reflectivity-based features. These properties make TravKAN attractive for robotic systems requiring transparency, real-time computational efficiency, and interpretability in safety-critical decision-making.

</details>


### [96] [ChainVLA: Chaining Vision-Language-Action Queries through a Unified Execution State for Long-Horizon Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02326)
*Yuzhi Huang,Weijue Bu,Ziyi Xiong,Jie Wu,Fanding Huang,Jingyan Jiang,Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ChainVLA 提出了一种链式 VLA 策略，通过可修正的联合执行状态（Progress Context + Motion Tail）将连续查询衔接起来，在 RMBench 和 LIBERO 上取得显著提升，消融实验表明两个组件缺一不可。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类在执行长时序操作时，会保留先前动作建立的任务进展知识，同时持续适应当前运动。但现有 VLA 策略每次查询都从当前输入重新规划，已有的记忆方法保留长期任务证据、动作复用方法保留短期运动，二者未能实现跨查询的完整衔接。

Method: ChainVLA（1.2B 参数）通过两个组件实现链式查询衔接：(1) Progress Context：将循环工作状态（Working State）与稀疏事件记忆结合，传递从观测中提取的任务进展；(2) Motion Tail：将上一条预测中未执行的动作延续送入状态构建和动作生成。二者共同条件化解码器，在最新观测下重新生成每个动作时域，使传递的状态引导而非固定下一次预测。

Result: ChainVLA 在 RMBench 上平均成功率达 62.8%，在四个 LIBERO 套件上达 98.8%。消融实验中，移除 Motion Tail 后 RMBench 成功率降至 11.2%，移除 Progress Context 降至 3.0%，呈现不对称的显著下降。

Conclusion: 运动连续性有助于保护观测流，从而支撑任务进展的推断；Progress Context 和 Motion Tail 的协同设计是实现跨查询平滑衔接、提升长时序操控成功率的关键。

Abstract: Humans perform long-horizon manipulation by retaining knowledge of what earlier actions have established while continuously adapting the motion underway. By contrast, action-chunked vision-language-action (VLA) policies repeatedly replan from the current input at each query. Existing methods preserve either long-term task evidence through memory or short-term motion through action reuse and ensembling, leaving the cross-query handoff incomplete. We introduce ChainVLA, a 1.2B-parameter VLA policy that chains successive queries through a joint and revisable execution state. Progress Context combines a recurrent Working State with sparse event memory to carry observation-derived task progress, while Motion Tail feeds the preceding prediction's unexecuted continuation into state construction and action generation. Together, the two components condition a decoder that regenerates each action horizon under the latest observation, allowing the carried state to guide the next prediction without fixing it. ChainVLA reaches 62.8% average success on RMBench and 98.8% across four LIBERO suites, while removing Motion Tail or Progress Context reduces RMBench success to 11.2% and 3.0%, respectively. These asymmetric ablations are consistent with motion continuity helping preserve the observation stream from which task progress is inferred.

</details>


### [97] [StableMimic: Smooth Human-Like Recovery for Humanoid Motion Tracking - Learning Beyond the Tracking Distribution for Structured Post-Fall Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02385)
*Weihao Wu,Ming Huang,Ruofei Liu,Jinglei Nie,Shuxiang Guo,Chunying Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: StableMimic 是一种统一的人形机器人运动跟踪器，通过在标称跟踪分布之外进行训练，使机器人具备从摔倒等低高度、接触密集状态中自主恢复的能力，并在跟踪精度和恢复安全性上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人形运动跟踪器仅在标称运动分布内表现可靠。一旦机器人摔倒，进入低姿态、多接触状态，跟踪策略会追逐不可达的参考轨迹，产生快速大幅的肢体修正，增加机器人及环境的风险。因此需要一种既能跟踪又能自主恢复的统一策略。

Method: 提出 StableMimic，核心包括：(1) 围绕多种人类起身参考动作进行扰动重置，覆盖俯卧、仰卧、失衡及中间接地状态，塑造结构化恢复行为；(2) 采用专家混合架构，为跟踪和恢复两种分布分别设置专用专家，并通过本体感知门控连续融合其动作；(3) 使用隐藏后继状态目标（hidden successor-state objective）来教授类人恢复行为，部署时无需暴露参考身份或相位，无需起身参考、恢复指令、轨迹检索或外部策略切换。

Result: 在完整 LAFAN1 舞蹈子集上，StableMimic 在四项跟踪指标上均取得五种方法中最低的误差。在每种方法各 100 次推倒试验中，StableMimic 以 100/100 的恢复成功率，在七项摔倒后运动与负载指标中的六项上取得最优值。在宇树 G1 机器人上的实际部署中，舞蹈跟踪和站立恢复场景均展示了有限肢体运动、自主恢复和指令恢复能力。

Conclusion: StableMimic 通过在训练中引入摔倒恢复分布并采用专家混合与后继状态目标，成功统一了运动跟踪与自主恢复，无需额外推理时组件，显著提升了人形机器人运动的安全性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Humanoid motion trackers perform reliably within learned tracking distributions, but falls can move the robot into low-height, contact-rich states from which an advancing command is temporarily unreachable. Tracking-only policies may chase infeasible references, producing rapid, large-amplitude limb corrections that increase risk to the robot and its surroundings. We present StableMimic, a unified tracker trained beyond the nominal tracking distribution. Perturbed resets around multiple human get-up references expose prone, supine, off-balance, and intermediate ground-contact states, shaping structured recovery that returns the robot to the trackable region. Because tracking and recovery occupy markedly different state--action distributions, StableMimic uses dedicated experts for each regime and a proprioceptive gate that continuously blends their actions. A hidden successor-state objective teaches human-reference-shaped recovery without exposing reference identity or phase to the deployed Actor; deployment requires no get-up reference, recovery command, trajectory retrieval, or external policy switch. On the complete retargeted LAFAN1 dance subset, StableMimic achieves the lowest errors on all four tracking metrics among five methods. Across 100 matched push-to-fall trials per method, it recovers in 100/100 and attains the lowest values on six of seven post-fall motion and load measures, supporting improved interaction safety under this protocol. Real Unitree G1 dance and standing-reference deployments qualitatively demonstrate bounded limb motion, autonomous recovery, and command resumption.

</details>


### [98] [Environmental resilience via morphological diversity within machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02395)
*Alice Hein,Josh Bongard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个机制：通过物理连接器将多个形态各异的智能体拴在一起形成集体，连接器在学习恢复被扰乱行为的过程中，会触发并驯服足够多样化的内部扰动，使得后续面对新环境时产生的外部扰动落在此前已被驯服的范围内，从而使集体无需额外学习即可保持正常行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物体具有跨尺度的感知运动部件并能快速适应新环境，而机器在较小尺度上仅含惰性材料且难以应对意外。作者假设生物体"智能体中嵌套智能体"的特性可能有助于其韧性：经历内部物理逆境可以预训练生物体和机器应对外部逆境。然而这一假设此前未被明确提出，相关机制也未被提出。

Method: 通过物理连接器将多个先前独立、形态多样的智能体拴在一起，连接器通过学习来恢复因拴连而被扰乱的行为。在此过程中，连接器触发并驯服足够多样化的内部扰动，使集体获得应对新环境中外部扰动的能力，无需额外学习或适应。进一步实验通过增加智能体数量或多样性来测试对集体韧性的影响。

Result: 物理连接器在学习恢复行为的过程中，驯服了足够多样化的内部扰动，使得后续新环境中的外部扰动落入可管理范围，集体无需额外学习即可正常运作。此外，由更多智能体或更多样化智能体构建的集体，对新环境的韧性更强。

Conclusion: 不仅驯服、而且有意制造内部物理逆境，确实可以帮助生物体准备应对外部逆境；若机器由更小的机器组成，这一机制同样适用于机器。

Abstract: Organisms contain diverse, sensorimotor parts across size scales and rapidly adapt to new environments, while machines contain only inert materials at smaller scales and struggle with surprise. We hypothesize that this agents-within-agents quality of organisms may aid their resilience: increasing experiences with internal physical adversity may pre-train organisms and machines to handle external adversity, such as encounters with new environments. Not only has this hypothesis not yet been articulated, mechanisms enabling this phenomenon have yet to be proposed. Here we show a mechanism by which this can occur: we found that physical connectors, in learning to restore behavior to previously independent, morphologically diverse agents they disrupted by tethering them together, trigger and tame sufficiently diverse disruptions that later encounters with new environments trigger disruptions that fall within this manageable range, enabling the collective to continue behaving properly without any additional learning or adaptation. Further, we found that building collectives from more agents, or more diverse agents, further increases the collective's resilience to new environments. This suggests that not just taming but intentionally creating internal physical adversity may indeed prepare organisms for external adversity, and could do so for machines, if they were built from smaller machines.

</details>


### [99] [Human-Centered Reflections on Care Robots: A Comparative Study of Caregiver Perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02411)
*Laura Londoño,Klaus Baumann,Abhinav Valada,Markus Langer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究通过混合方法调查了美国、墨西哥和智利298名护理人员对四类护理机器人的接受度，发现各国参与者普遍对物流配送和体力辅助型机器人评价积极，而对需高强度人际互动的机器人持保留态度；定性分析进一步揭示了关于工作量减轻、患者自主性提升等益处，以及对可靠性、人工监督和就业替代的伦理关切，且不同国家在伦理关切的解读和优先级上存在差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着护理机器人日益进入医疗场景，其接受度和伦理实施成为关键问题。现有研究缺乏跨国比较视角，且未充分结合定量接受度模型与定性伦理分析，难以全面理解不同文化背景下护理人员对多类别护理机器人的感知差异。

Method: 采用混合方法研究，使用混合因子设计。298名来自美国、墨西哥和智利的护理人员评估了四类护理机器人（物资配送、协助上床、生命体征监测、行动辅助）。定量部分整合UTAUT模型、CAN模型和总体接受度评分；定性部分通过开放式问题收集数据，并基于文献驱动的伦理框架进行分析。

Result: 三国参与者普遍对护理机器人持积极评价，尤其偏好物流和体力密集型任务，而非高强度人际互动任务。定性结果显示，参与者强调减少工作量、降低风险和增强患者自主性等潜在益处，同时表达了对可靠性、人工监督必要性和潜在就业替代的担忧。虽然多数伦理关切为各国共有，但在解读和优先排序上存在跨国差异。

Conclusion: 研究推进了对护理机器人负责任设计和实施的情境敏感、社会知情式理解，表明在推广护理机器人时需充分考虑任务类型、文化背景和伦理维度的差异，为跨文化医疗场景中的机器人部署提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Care robots are increasingly being introduced into healthcare settings, raising important questions about their acceptance and ethical implementation. To better understand these challenges, this study investigates caregivers' perceptions of four categories of care robots: delivering supplies, helping patients into bed, monitoring vital signs, and assisting with mobility. We conducted a mixed-methods study employing a mixed-factorial design in which 298 caregivers from the United States, Mexico, and Chile evaluated all four robot categories. Quantitative measures integrated constructs from the Unified Theory of Acceptance and Use of Technology, the Cognitive-Affective-Normative model, and overall acceptance ratings. Qualitative data were collected through open-ended questions and analyzed using a literature-informed ethical framework. The results indicate that participants across countries generally evaluated care robots positively, particularly for logistical and physically demanding tasks rather than those requiring intensive interpersonal interaction. The qualitative findings provide further insight into stakeholders' views of the ethical implications of care robot use. Participants emphasized potential benefits such as reduced workload, lower risk, and greater patient autonomy, while also expressing concerns about dependability, the need for human oversight, and potential job displacement. Although many ethical concerns were shared across countries, participants differed in how they interpreted and prioritized them. These findings advance a context-sensitive and socially informed understanding of responsible design and implementation of care robots.

</details>


### [100] [Certifying Plans under Model Mismatch: A Trilemma for Reachability from Scarce Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02453)
*Yanliang Huang,Zhen Zhang,Ahmad Hafez,Wenyuan Wu,Peng Xie,Zhuoqi Zeng,Amr Alanwar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究仿真到现实(sim-to-real)控制策略的预执行认证问题，揭示了轨迹包容性、有限投影宽度和无限制模型误差行为之间的三难困境，并提出了ForeReach方法：基于组件级Lipschitz假设构建模型误差的集合隶属包络，通过zonotopic可达管传播进行安全认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真训练的策略在真实系统部署时面临名义动力学与目标系统动力学之间的模型误差。目标系统通常只能提供少量孤立的一步转移数据，当控制序列进入未观测的状态-输入区域时，任何确定性认证器都面临根本性困境：要么拒绝认证，要么返回投影宽度任意大的可达管。这驱动作者形式化分析这一困境并寻求在有限数据下的实用认证方案。

Method: 首先，在理论层面分析了认证三难困境（trilemma）：均匀轨迹包容、有限投影宽度、以及观测外无限制模型误差行为三者无法同时成立，并推导了有限规划相关的投影宽度下界。然后提出ForeReach方法：要求用户提供模型误差的组件级Lipschitz常数声明；利用观测到的转移对验证该声明（可证伪但不可证实）；在声明有效的条件下，构建模型误差的集合隶属包络，利用zonotopic集合表示进行可达性传播；仅当传播全程保持在认证域内且每个投影管切片均避开不安全集时才予以认证。

Result: 在两个基准系统上的实验表明：传统校准基线在数据支持范围外虽保持窄的可达管但丧失了轨迹包容性（不安全），而ForeReach正确地拒绝认证数据支持不足的控制序列，并能在获得相关目标数据和足够障碍物间隙时恢复认证能力。

Conclusion: 仿真到现实部署中的安全认证面临数据稀缺导致的根本性困难。通过组件级Lipschitz结构假设，ForeReach在观测数据支持范围内提供有保障的认证，超出支持范围则保守地拒绝认证，在数据覆盖与安全保证之间取得了原则性的平衡。

Abstract: Sim-to-real policies are designed under nominal dynamics, but target-system trials may yield only a few isolated one-step transitions. We study pre-execution certification of a fixed control sequence, such as an action chunk produced by a learned policy. If the sequence reaches an unobserved state-input region, the observations remain consistent with target systems whose trajectories separate along it by an arbitrarily large amount. Any deterministic certifier sound for all of them must then decline to certify or return a reachable tube with arbitrarily large projected width. For bounded smooth classes of the target-nominal model error, we derive a finite plan-dependent projected-width lower bound. These results expose a trilemma among uniform trajectory containment, finite projected width, and unrestricted model-error behavior beyond the observations. ForeReach requires a supplied componentwise Lipschitz bound on the model error. Observed transition pairs can refute this declaration but cannot establish it outside the observed locations. Conditional on a valid declaration, our method constructs a set-membership envelope for the model error, propagates a zonotopic reachable tube, and certifies only when propagation remains within the certification domain and every projected tube slice avoids the unsafe set. In two benchmark systems, calibration baselines may remain narrow after losing trajectory containment outside data support, whereas our method declines to certify unsupported sequences and recovers certification when relevant target data and sufficient obstacle clearance are available.

</details>


### [101] [Grounded Semantic Re-Binding for Robust Instruction Generalization in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02497)
*Zhaokai Yin,Zhipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLA模型因架构缺陷在指令改写时性能骤降，本文提出GSR方法，通过解耦语义与视觉特征并重新训练动作专家，在无需数据扩增的情况下显著恢复鲁棒性，并推出原生解耦模型ParaVLA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA模型对指令改写极其脆弱，传统方案依赖昂贵的数据扩增来缓解，但作者通过探针分析发现根因并非语义理解不足，而是架构层面的联合编码导致特征偏移，使下游动作策略无法将正确的语义转化为控制指令。

Method: 提出Grounded Semantic Re-binding (GSR)方法：独立提取任务语义与原生视觉特征，显式融合后从头训练一个完全重新初始化的动作专家，从而绕过不稳定的联合路由瓶颈。并基于此构建原生解耦的ParaVLA模型（0.33B参数）。

Result: 在LIBERO-Para基准上，GSR将成功率提升高达44.6%；轻量模型可媲美大规模基线；将SOTA模型的PRIDE分数推至70.4（超越小米机器人）；ParaVLA对指令改写展现近乎完美的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 鲁棒的语义 grounding 可以通过优雅的结构设计而非暴力数据扩增实现，GSR和ParaVLA证明了这一范式的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models excel in robotic manipulation but suffer catastrophic performance drops when canonical instructions are simply paraphrased. Although this brittleness is typically addressed through costly data scaling, our probing reveals that the root cause is architectural rather than a lack of semantic understanding. Specifically, we demonstrate that current VLAs successfully retain the correct task identity internally. The failure actually stems from the joint encoding of dynamic visual observations and text, which introduces systematic feature shifts. Because the downstream action policy is highly vulnerable to these variations, it fails to translate the preserved semantics into correct control commands. To resolve this structural bottleneck, we propose Grounded Semantic Re-binding (GSR), an elegant intervention that bypasses unstable joint routing by explicitly fusing independently extracted task semantics with native visual features to train a completely re-initialized action expert from scratch. This targeted intervention dramatically restores paraphrastic invariance using only canonical demonstrations. On the LIBERO-Para benchmark, GSR improves success rates by up to 44.6 percent. It enables lightweight models to rival massively scaled baselines and pushes state-of-the-art models to a new record PRIDE score of 70.4, outperforming the recently introduced large-scale pretrained model Xiaomi-Robotics-0 in instruction generation capabilities. Building on these insights, we also introduce ParaVLA, a natively decoupled 0.33B-parameter model exhibiting near-perfect robustness to instruction rewording. Ultimately, our work proves that robust semantic grounding can be achieved through elegant structural design, bypassing the inefficient brute-force data scaling paradigm.

</details>


### [102] [Probabilistic Reachable-Action Verification of Visuomotor Policies via Set-Based Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02545)
*Yanliang Huang,Zhuocheng Zhang,Peng Xie,Zhen Zhang,Wenyuan Wu,Majid Khadiv,Zhuoqi Zeng,Amr Alanwar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对视觉运动策略的可达性分析方法，通过冻结视觉编码器、在低维接口进行集合传播，结合集合训练和保形校准，在保证闭环任务能力的同时有效缩小动作偏差半径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉运动策略的可达性分析面临核心挑战：大型视觉编码器使得端到端的集合传播计算代价高昂且过度保守。需要一种既高效又能提供概率保证的方法。

Method: （1）冻结视觉编码器，将集合传播限制在编码器与下游策略之间的低维接口，接口集合通过留出的相机位姿扰动进行校准；（2）使用zonotope（区域体）通过策略传播该集合，得到终端输出包络宽度，由基于集合的训练直接优化；（3）评估阶段从指定分布采样相机位姿扰动，利用rollout级别的分裂保形校准（split conformal calibration），将动作偏差分数转换为具有有限样本覆盖保证的概率可达动作半径。

Result: 在受控操作实验中，基于集合的训练缩小了可达动作半径，同时保持了闭环任务能力；相比之下，仅行为匹配、观测一致性匹配和逐点对抗训练等对照组均产生更大的半径。

Conclusion: 通过将集合传播限制在低维接口并结合集合训练与保形校准，可以在不牺牲任务性能的前提下，获得更紧致且具有概率保证的可达性分析结果。

Abstract: Reachability analysis for visuomotor policies is difficult because large visual encoders make end-to-end set propagation computationally expensive and excessively conservative. We therefore freeze the visual encoder and confine set propagation to a low-dimensional interface between it and the downstream policy, with the interface set calibrated from held-out camera-pose perturbations. Propagating this set through the policy with zonotopes yields a terminal output-enclosure width that set-based training optimizes directly. During evaluation, camera-pose perturbations are sampled from the prescribed distribution, and rollout-level split conformal calibration converts the resulting action-deviation scores into a probabilistic reachable-action radius with finite-sample coverage. In controlled manipulation experiments, set-based training reduces this radius while preserving closed-loop task capability, and matched behavior-only, observational-consistency, and pointwise-adversarial controls all leave a larger radius.

</details>


### [103] [Why Does Action Chunking Improve Behavioral Cloning Performance in Robotic Control?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02547)
*Filippo Lazzati,Kyle Stachowicz,William Chen,Alberto Maria Metelli,Andrew Wagenmaker,Sergey Levine*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文揭示动作分块（action chunking）成功的关键并非先前假设的时间一致性、视野缩减或表征学习，而是非马尔可夫表达力、降低的复合误差，以及一种被称为"隐式集成"的新机制——动作分块策略隐式学习了多种时间关系，表现出类似模型集成的行为。基于此，作者提出通过随机延迟策略集成即可匹敌动作分块性能，并进一步提出显式集成策略类以放大该优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作分块（预测并执行多个动作）已被证明是学习有效机器人控制策略的关键技术，但其为何有效的精确机理尚不清楚。本文旨在填补这一理解空白。

Method: 通过在仿真和真实世界环境下进行严格的实验评估，系统检验了关于动作分块成功的现有假说（时间一致性、视野缩减、表征学习），并引入延迟策略进行对照分析，揭示隐式集成效应。进一步提出随机延迟策略集成方法和显式集成策略类来验证和放大该效应。

Result: 现有假说均无法解释动作分块的成功；动作分块的优势主要来自更强的非马尔可夫表达力和更低的复合误差，但这些效果可被延迟策略复现。此外，动作分块存在额外的"隐式集成"优势——策略因学习多种时间关系而表现出集成模型的鲁棒性和泛化能力。在仿真和真实机器人控制中，用随机延迟策略集成可匹配动作分块性能；显式集成策略类则在多个领域显著超越动作分块。

Conclusion: 动作分块成功的核心机制是隐式集成效应，而非先前普遍认为的其他因素。通过将动作分块策略部署为随机延迟策略的集成，无需实际执行动作分块即可匹配其性能；而显式构建集成策略则可进一步超越动作分块的表现。

Abstract: Action chunking---predicting and executing multiple actions instead of a single action---has proven to be a critical component for learning effective robotic control policies. However, our precise understanding of why action chunking improves performance has remained limited. In this work we seek to close this gap. Through rigorous experimental evaluations in both simulated and real-world settings, we show that existing hypotheses for the success of action chunking---temporal consistency, horizon reduction, and representation learning---fail to explain the success of action chunking. Instead, we find that action chunking benefits from greater non-Markovian expressivity and reduced compounding error compared to Markovian policies, but, in many settings of interest, these effects can be fully captured by delayed policies, which at each step predict a single action based on the observation $k$ steps in the past. We then show that there exists an additional benefit of action chunking that we refer to as implicit ensembling. In particular, by learning a diversity of temporal relationships (that is, $a_t | o_t, a_t | o_{t-1}, \ldots$), action-chunked policies exhibit behavior matching that of a model ensemble, increasing their robustness and generalization ability over policies that only learn a single temporal relationship. Building on these insights, we show that in simulated and real-world robotic control settings, we can match the performance of action chunking without action chunking---by deploying an action chunking policy as an ensemble of policies with randomized delays. Furthermore, we propose a policy class that amplifies the benefits of action chunking by explicitly instantiating an ensemble, and which we show significantly improves over the performance of action chunking in many domains.

</details>
